# Project: the Laws of Hammurabi (prologue, laws, epilogue) → Hebrew

Corpus: `../corpus.md` (law and treaty), project 2. Read `../../translation-guidelines.md`, `../corpus.md`, `../renderings.md` (especially §5, the shared law terms) and this folder's `renderings.md` first.

## Scope
- **The whole text of the Louvre stele as restored in the CDLI composite**: the prologue (composite lines 1–303), the laws §1–§65 and §100 (end)–§282 (304–3143′), and the epilogue (3144′–3641′): **3,641 composite lines**.
- **The gap** (the seven erased columns of the stele's obverse): the composite has no text for §66–§99 and the beginning of §100 (only 1121′ = the restored last word of §65, and 1122′ `[...]`). These "gap laws" are known from other copies (OB tablets from Nippur, Sippar and elsewhere; numbered §66–§99 by Driver & Miles and Borger, "gap ¶ a …" by Roth 1997). **No open base text was found** (`../../sources/_records-only--Hammurabi-gap-laws-and-Hebrew/README.md`), so they are **not translated** in this round; the translation marks the gap (guidelines §6.8: `[…]` with a note), and the decision is open for the user.
- Old Babylonian Akkadian, c. 1755 BCE (Hammurabi's later years; the stele from Sippar, carried to Susa in the 12th century BCE).

## Base edition and sources
- **Rank 1:** CDLI **P464358** (RIME 4.03.06.add21 composite): transliteration, CDLI normalization (`#tr.ts`) on every line. `../../sources/CDLI/P464358--Laws-of-Hammurabi-composite/` (README). Readings checked against the **Louvre stele P249253** (aligned line by line by script: `P464358--composite-with-stele.md`; 292 lines differ, mostly stele damage that the composite fills from duplicates) and, for the prologue and epilogue, against **SEAL 7520** (Streck: transliteration, lemmas; `../../sources/SEAL/CH-Prologue-Epilogue--node-7520/`, gitignored).
- **Rank 2:** CDA (eBL dictionary API, per word), CAD (per word only, guidelines §8), SEAL's lemmas, Sefaria (Klein).
- **Rank 3 (after our own reading):** CDLI's English (on every line; translator not named); SEAL's English (Streck) for the prologue and epilogue.
- **Rank 5:** Harper 1904 (`../../sources/Harper-1904--Code-of-Hammurabi/`, public domain), history of interpretation only.
- **Not obtained** (record): Roth 1997 (SBL WAW 6), Richardson 2000/2004, Driver & Miles 1952–55, Finet 2002, Borger; the comparative studies (Paul 1970, Finkelstein 1981, Westbrook 1988/2003, Wright 2009, Jackson 2006, Otto). The parallels below that come from this literature are cited **from memory** of the standard discussion and marked so; parallels not known to us from the literature are marked "הצעה שלנו" (our proposal).

## Line numbering
- **Composite lines** as in CDLI: 1–1120, then 1121′–3641′ (the prime marks the lines after the gap; it is part of the label, `### 1130'`).
- **Laws** by the standard § numbers (Scheil → Roth 1997, as in CDLI): `## §N (first–last line): Hebrew heading`. §242–243 is one block in the composite (`## §242–243`). The lines 1123′–1129′, which CDLI still labels "law 65", are the end of §100.
- **Stele references** (`obv. 5:25`, `rev. 24:1`) are given in the data files and, where the stele matters, in the apparatus field **סטלה**. SEAL's columns use the continuous numbering (I–XXIII obverse, XXIV–LI reverse): SEAL col. i–v = obv. 1–5; col. xlvii–li = rev. 24–28.

## Parallels with the Bible to watch for (preliminary; each translator checks them)
- **Exodus 21–23, the Covenant Code** (verse by verse): 21:2–11 (debt slavery ↔ §117–119; the slave-wife ↔ §119, §146–147, §170–171); 21:6 (the ear ↔ §282, §205); 21:12–14 (homicide, intent ↔ §206–208, §1); 21:15, 17 (striking / cursing a parent ↔ §195, §192–193); 21:16 (kidnapping ↔ §14); 21:18–19 (the brawl, healing costs ↔ §206); 21:20–21, 26–27 (striking a slave ↔ §199, §213–214); 21:22–25 (the pregnant woman, talion ↔ §209–214, §196–201); 21:28–32 (the goring ox ↔ §250–252); 21:33–36 (pit, ox gores ox ↔ §245–249, cf. Eshnunna §53); 21:37–22:3 (theft of animals, the night burglar ↔ §8, §21–22, §265); 22:4–5 (grazing, fire ↔ §57–58, §25); 22:6–14 (deposit, oath before God, loss, herding, hire ↔ §7, §120–126, §244–249, §261–267); 22:15–16 (the seduced virgin, bride-price ↔ §130, §156); 22:17 (sorceress ↔ §2); 22:20–26 (widow and orphan, interest, pledge ↔ prologue/epilogue, §88–§96 (gap), §113–116); 23:1–3, 6–8 (false witness, judges, bribery ↔ §1–5); 23:20–33 (the closing promise ↔ the epilogue).
- **Elsewhere in the Torah:** Lev 24:17–22 (talion, "one law for the stranger") ↔ §196–201; Num 5:11–31 (the suspected wife) ↔ §131–132; Deut 19:16–21 (the false witness) ↔ §1–4; Deut 21:1–9 (the unsolved murder) ↔ §23–24; Deut 21:15–17 (the firstborn) ↔ §165–170; Deut 21:18–21 ↔ §168–169; Deut 22:13–29 ↔ §129–130, §142–143, §155–156; Deut 24:1–4 ↔ §137–141; Deut 24:7 ↔ §14; Deut 24:16 (no vicarious punishment) ↔ §116, §210, §230; Lev 18 and 20 (incest) ↔ §154–158; Lev 21:9 (the priest's daughter burnt) ↔ §110; Lev 25:39–43 ↔ §117; Gen 16, 21, 30 (the slave-wife and her children) ↔ §144–147, §170–171; Gen 31:38–40 (Jacob as herdsman) ↔ §261–267; Gen 38 ↔ §155–156; Gen 48:5 ↔ §170 (adoption by declaration); Ex 23:20–33, Lev 26, Deut 27–28 (blessings and curses; the law written on stone) ↔ the epilogue; Deut 17:18–20 (the king's long reign under the law) ↔ the epilogue's blessing; Ps 72; Isa 11:3–4 ↔ the prologue's just king.

## Decisions (coordinator, before drafting)
1. ***šumma* = `אם`** throughout (corpus §5; F1): no `כי` / `אם` hierarchy is imposed.
2. **Class terms**: *awīlum* = `איש`, *mār awīlim* = `בן איש` (Ps 49:3 `בְּנֵי אִישׁ`), *muškēnum* = `מִסְכֵּן` (the Hebrew loanword from it), *wardum* = `עבד`, *amtum* = `אמה` – always distinct, so that every tier is visible. A deviation from the flood and creation corpora (*awīlu* = `אָדָם` in narrative), recorded in corpus §5.
3. ***iddâk* = `יהרג`**, never `יומת` (corpus *dâku* = `הָרַג`): the Torah's `מוֹת יוּמָת` is [equivalent], not echoed.
4. ***maḫar ilim* = `לפני האל`** (never `אלהים`) ↔ `אֶל הָאֱלֹהִים` (Ex 22:7–8) [equivalent].
5. ***nakāpu* = `נגף`**, ***nakkāpû* = `נַגָּף`** (the cognate of Ex 21:35 `יִגֹּף`; the noun pattern of `נַגָּח`, another root); ***šūdû* = `הודיע`** (cognate ידע) ↔ `וְהוּעַד` [equivalent].
6. ***kīma* = `כ`** in the "X for X" clauses (`שור כשור`, `עבד כעבד`); `תחת` kept out.
7. ***mīšarum* = `מישרים`** [cognate], *kittum* = `אמת`; *dīnum* = `דין` [cognate], *dīnāt mīšarim* = `דיני מישרים`; `משפט`, `צדקה` kept out (corpus §5).
8. ***šībū* = `עדים`** (note: "elders", `שָׂב`); *šībūt sarrātim* = `עדות שקרים` (the proposed cognate `סָרָה`, Deut 19:16, in the note only – open decision).
9. **Curse formulas of the epilogue**: EST's Hebrew is reused where the Akkadian is identical in form (*arratam maruštam … līrur* = `מארה נמרצת … יאור`; *šumšu zēršu … ḫulluqu* = `את שמו ואת זרעו … יאבד`; *qaštu* / *šebēru* = `קשת` / `שבר`; *mūtānu* = `מגפה`; *bubūtu* = `רעב`; corpus §4). The epilogue translator greps EST's `>` lines for every curse phrase.
10. **Reserved words** (project `renderings.md` §4) are kept out of the `>` lines and checked by `verify.py`.
11. **Hammurabi** = `חַמּוּרַבִּי` (familiar); *Ḫammu-rāpi* and its etymology in the note (open decision).
12. **Gap laws** not translated (no open base text): open decision.

## Working rules for the translators
- **Sequential**: one translator at a time, in the order of the plan below; each reads the `>` lines of all earlier files (grep them) and keeps every repeated formula identical (renderings §1).
- **Draft unvocalized, plene, from the first line** (guidelines §5). Vocalize names at their first occurrence in the file, ambiguous words, and the words listed in renderings §1–§2 as "vocalized".
- **Parse every word** in the **פירוק** field: lemma, stem (G/D/Š/N/Gt/Gtn…), tense (preterite / perfect / present / stative), person, ventive, subjunctive, *-ma*. For a pure formula line (`šumma awīlum`, `inaddin`, `iddâk` …) the field may just say "נוסחה F1 (renderings)".
- **Read the stele line** (`S:` in the data) and record in **סטלה** when the stele is damaged where the composite restores, or reads differently; for the prologue and epilogue read **SEAL** too (its readings and lemmas; SEAL's lemmas have slips: AN in l. 1 is Anum, not *šamû*).
- **Consult CDLI's English (and SEAL's) only after forming the reading**; record disagreements in **תרגומים**.
- **Echo check on every line**; keep the reserved words out; tag every Bible overlap in **הקבלה** with [cognate] / [equivalent] / [divergence] / [absent], quoting the verse vocalized in backticks with its reference (checked by `tools/check_bible_quotes.py`); "מן הזיכרון" for parallels from the literature, "הצעה שלנו" for ours.
- Each translator ends with a **Part section** in this file: scope, decisions, open decisions for the user, progress, **parallels index table**, proposals for the renderings, lessons.

### Annotated file format (`annotated-NN--<slug>.rtl.md`, lh_config.FILES)
```markdown
## §117 (1324'–1337'): מכירה בחוב, ודרור בשנה הרביעית
### 1324'
`szum-ma a-wi-lam e-'i3-il-tum`
`N: šumma awīlam e’iltum`
> אם איש תפשתו חובה,
- **פירוק:** …
- **סטלה:** … (optional)
- **תרגומים:** CDLI: "…"; SEAL (Streck): "…" (prologue/epilogue)
- **הקבלה:** [equivalent] `כִּי תִקְנֶה עֶבֶד עִבְרִי שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים יַעֲבֹד` (שמות כא:ב) …
- **הערות:** …
- **הערת שוליים:** (optional: becomes a footnote of the clean translation; only for the few points a reader must know)
```
- Section headings: laws `## §N (first–last): heading`; prologue and epilogue `## פרולוג (1–49): heading`, `## אפילוג (3144'–3239'): heading`; the gap `## הפער (1122'): §66–§99 ותחילת §100`.
- Entry headers `### <composite line>` exactly as in the edition; one `>` line per entry; the transliteration and the normalization in backticks.
- A file whose laws are drafted in two rounds keeps the not-yet-drafted laws as one comment line `<!-- טרם תורגם: §6–§13 -->` in their place.

### Parallels index format (in each Part section below)
`| Line | Original | Hebrew rendering | Bible | Tag | Comment |` — Line = `§N, line` (e.g. `§250, 2883'`) or `prol. 32`; the Bible column starts with a backticked, vocalized quotation followed by its reference `(שמות כא:כח)`; one row per (line, verse) pair; a restored basis is said in the Comment.

## Status
**Complete (first draft), 2026-10-17**: all **3,641 composite lines** – the prologue (1–303), §1–§65, the end of §100 and §101–§282 (304–3143′; the gap §66–§99 marked, not translated), the epilogue (3144′–3641′) – in twelve annotated files (`annotated-01--prologue.rtl.md` … `annotated-12--epilogue-2.rtl.md`, lh_config.FILES), drafted by twelve sequential translators (T1–T12, Parts below) in the priority order of the plan (prologue + epilogue; the priority laws §1–5, 14–25, 117–126, 129–132, 144–158, 195–214, 229–231, 244–252, 261–267; then all the rest), each with three review passes; coordinator harmonization (below). `verify.py --strict`: 3,641 entries, 0 not drafted, 0 problems (40 "identical normalization, different Hebrew" items reviewed: all are punctuation, the conjunction from a preceding *-ma*, gender/number of the referent, or the referent rule of *bēlšu*); `tools/check_bible_quotes.py`: 0 mismatches on every file; `translation.rtl.md` built by `build_translation.py` (header `build_translation--head.rtl.md`; topic headings from `lh_config.TOPICS`; 130 footnotes); 1,263 parallels rows merged into `../parallels-index.md` (corpus builder `../build_parallels_index.py`) with the key findings there; print stub `../../הדפסה/חוקים-ובריתות/2-חוקי-חמורבי.rtl.md`.
To rebuild the translators' data: `python3 -I build_data.py <scratch-dir>`.

### Coordinator harmonization (2026-10-17)
- *mâtu* Š (*uštamīt*) = `המית` (reserved; nine occurrences, the cognate of `וְהֵמִית`, Exod 21:29); T5's 1812' (*dâku* Š) changed to `את בעלה (בידי אחר) הרגה`.
- *ṣābum* = `צבא` [cognate] (731, and 3629′ changed from T3's `גדודיו`); *ummānum* = `חיל`.
- `משפט` allowed only for *šipṭu* (3429′); `מהומה`, `מטר`, `שחין`, `קללה` added to the reserved list (renderings §4; `verify.py`).
- F22: *mimmûm* = `רכוש` (T4). F26 as drafted: `בדעת לא מחצתי`. Vocalizations in renderings §2–3 made plene (`שׁוּגִּיתוּ`, `כּוּלְמַשִׁיתוּ`, `אוּגְבַּבְתּוּ`, `תּוּתּוּל`).
- `verify.py` extended by the translators (F1 with *šū*, feminine forms, `ישל[םמ]`, *iriābbū*) and the coordinator (`משפט`/*šipṭu*, `נגף` only with *nakāpu*, short-vowel spellings).
- Left as documented collisions (open decisions 9–11 below): `המיר` for *enû* (T4) and the noun `תמורה` for *pūḫum* (T8); `שבוי` (*turru*, T8) and `נשבה` (*šalālu* N, T10); `הרים` for *ana ilim našû* (T12) and *ullû* (T2); `נשא` for *aḫāzu* and for *wabālu* in two idioms (T11).

## Open decisions for the user
The most consequential (the Parts list the rest, with alternatives):
1. **The gap laws §66–§99** (and the beginning of §100) are not translated: no open base text (`../../sources/_records-only--Hammurabi-gap-laws-and-Hebrew/README.md`). A later session could use Driver & Miles / Roth (paid or library) or the PD page images of Kohler–Ungnad / Cuq 1918.
2. ***muškēnum* = `מִסְכֵּן`** (the Hebrew loanword; modern readers hear "pitiable") vs `הֶדְיוֹט` or a transliteration.
3. ***awīlum* = `איש`** (deviation from the narrative corpora's `אָדָם`; corpus renderings §5) and *mār awīlim* = `בן איש`.
4. **Hammurabi** = `חַמּוּרַבִּי` (familiar) vs `חַמּוּרָפִּי` (*Ḫammu-rāpi*).
5. ***šībūt sarrātim* = `עדות שקרים`**; the proposed cognate `סָרָה` (Deut 19:16; 13:6) only in the notes.
6. **§21 *iḫallalūšu*** (and §227): `יתלוהו?` ("hang") vs "wall in"; the Deut 21:22 parallel depends on it.
7. ***nakkāpû* = `נַגָּף`** (cognate root of Exod 21:35, pattern of `נַגָּח`) and *šūdû* = `הודיע`; *qarnīšu lā ušarrim* = `לא קִיטֵּם?`.
8. ***kibrātum* = `קצות הארץ`** (prologue); *raggam u ṣēnam* = `רשע וָזֵד`; `חברו` for the cognate *rū'u* (because `רעהו` is reserved).
9. `המיר` (*enû*) / `תמורה` (*pūḫum*); 10. `שבוי` / `נשבה`; 11. `הרים` (two verbs); 12. `ilkum` = `הֲלָךְ` (the Aramaic loanword of Ezra 4:13) vs `שירות`.
13. ***nudunnûm* = `מַתָּן`** (Gen 34:12), not `נדוניה` (which in later Hebrew is the father's dowry, *šeriktum* = `שִׁלּוּחִים`).
14. `קרא` for *šasû* "read aloud" (epilogue 3248′), although `קרא` is reserved for naming (*nabû*).
15. `בַּשּׁוּק` for *sūqum* (§250, "street"); `דמי שכירותו` for *idūšu*; `ארץ נכר` for *māt nukurtim*; `כפייה` for *bennum*.

## Part T1: the prologue (1–303)

### Scope
- `annotated-01--prologue.rtl.md`: composite lines **1–303**, all drafted (303 entries, one `>` line each), in eight sections: the gods' commission of Marduk and Babylon (1–26); Anu and Enlil name Hammurabi (27–49); the epithets city by city: Nippur, Eridu, Babylon (50–78); Ur, Sippar, Larsa, Uruk, Isin, Kiš (79–133); Kutha, Borsippa, Dilbat, Keš, Lagaš–Girsu, Zabala, Karkara (134–200); Adab, Maškan-šāpir, Malgium, the Euphrates, Mari and Tuttul, Eshnunna (Tišpak, Ninazu), Babylon (201–252); Akkad, Assur, Nineveh, the dynasty, "sun of Babylon", the four quarters (253–291); Marduk's command and *kittam u mīšaram … aškun* (292–303).
- The real boundaries: the *īnu* clause ends at 26 (*ukinnušum*); the main clause 27–49 ends with *šumī ibbû*; the nominal sentence *Ḫammurapi … anāku* runs 50–53 and its appositions to 291 (*migir Ištar anāku*); the closing *inūma* sentence 292–302; 303 *inūmišu* leads into §1. The Erra epithet (134–137) belongs to Kutha (138–142), so the Kiš section ends at 133.
- Base: CDLI P464358 `A:`/`N:`; stele P249253 (damaged only at 98, 142, 214, 295); SEAL 7520 transliteration and lemmas; CDLI English and Streck (SEAL) English read after drafting.

### Decisions (translator)
1. **One sentence, line by line.** The prologue is one long sentence; each Hebrew line renders its Akkadian line only, with commas/semicolons (semicolon at the end of each city block, as the cola end in the Akkadian). Participial epithets = Hebrew participle with the article (`המכונן`, `המשכלל`); *ša* = `אשר`; *šū* + subjunctive (209, 217, 237) = `הוא אשר`.
2. **Names** (first occurrence vocalized; plene plain forms): see Proposals §A. Temples transliterated with the meaning in a note. *Dur-an-ki* (59) is transliterated `דּוּר־אַנְקִי` (the stele has the Sumerian logogram), though CDLI's `N:` reads it *markas šamê u erṣetim*; `קשר` is avoided anyway (*bārtu* = `קשר`, corpus §2). *Emeslam* (142, 214): `ל⟨אֵ⟩מֶסְלַם` for the composite's `<e2>` (the stele and SEAL have *Meslam*). 244: CDLI's normalization drops *mākalī*, which the transliteration and the stele have: translated from the transliteration (`השׂם מאכלים טהורים`), said in **סטלה**.
3. ***kibrātum* = `קצות הארץ`** (the flood corpus's *kibrāti*; Enūma eliš VII), *kibrāt erbettim* (69–70) / *kibrāt arba'im* (289–290) = `קצות הארץ / הארבע` (the numeral in its own line). `ארבע כנפות הארץ` (Isa 11:12) kept for the apparatus, not to pre-empt the comparison.
4. ***nišū* = `האנשים`** (earlier corpora); `עם` is kept for ***ammu*** (262, the West-Semitic cognate, `המיישר את העם`). *šīr nišī ṭubbu* = `להיטיב את בשר האנשים` (47–48, 302), the idiom kept literally (Streck "well-being" in the note).
5. ***inūmīšu* = `בימים ההם`** (27, 303), *īnu* / *inūma* = `כאשר`. Deut 1:16, 18 `בָּעֵת הַהִוא` noted, not echoed.
6. ***nibīt* = `קרוא`**, *nabû* = `קרא` (17, 49 `בשמה הנעלה קראו`, `בשמי קראו`): Isa 45:3–4 visible, tagged [equivalent].
7. **The weak and the strong:** *dannum enšam ana lā ḫabālim* = `שהחזק / בחלש / לא יחבול` (37–39, F39 split as in the Akkadian). *dannu* = `חזק` also in the royal epithets (89, 277, 281). *raggam u ṣēnam* = `רשע וָזֵד` (35): `רע` is left for *lemnu* (corpus §0); `זד` makes Mal 3:19–20 (זדים … שמש צדקה) visible as an [equivalent] sequence, said in the apparatus.
8. **Šamaš / šamšu:** `כְּשַׁמַשׁ` (40), `לשַׁמַשׁ` (89, 101) vocalized at every occurrence, `שֶׁמֶשׁ` (282, *šamšu bābilim*). *waṣû* "rise" = `יצא` (42; the cognate, = Judg 5:31 `כְּצֵאת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ`), Š = `הוציא` (284).
9. ***šakānu* = `שׂם`** throughout (105, 198 Gtn, 244, 301): 301 `אמת / ומישרים / בפי הארץ / שַׂמְתִּי` keeps the verb distinct from Ps 99:4 `כּוֹנַנְתָּ` (*kunnu*) and makes Deut 31:19 `שִׂימָהּ בְּפִיהֶם` visible.
10. **Reserved word met as a real cognate:** *rū'u* "friend" (135) is the cognate of `רֵעַ`, but `רעהו` is reserved (§4): rendered `חברו`, the cognate in the note.
11. ***nakāpu* D** (145 *munakkip zā'irī*) = `הנוגף את השונאים` (F32's verb; the king as the wild ox, `הָרְאֵם`, Deut 33:17).
12. **Several "abundance" words, several Hebrew words:** *nuḫšu* `עתרת`, *ḫegallu* `שפע`, *ṭuḫdu* `דשן` (and *ṭaḫādu* D `דישן`), *ḫiṣbu* `תנובה` (`יבול` kept out). Heaping verbs: *kamāru* D `גדש`, *qarānu* D `ערם` (creation corpus).
13. **Exaltation verbs kept apart:** *ṣīru* `נעלה`; *ṣiāru* D `נישא` (95); *elû* D `הרים` (108, *rēša ullû*); *atāru* Š `רומם` (19, 138; the cognate `יֶתֶר` in the note, `הותיר` misleads); *rabû* Š `הגדיל` (15, 71, 227); *šadālu* D `השגיא`, *rapāšu* D `הרחיב`.
14. ***ḫaṭṭu* = `שבט`** (**changed to `שרביט` by the 2026-10-23 harmonization**, `harmonization--2026-10-23.md` row 4: `שבט` is reserved for its cognate *šabbiṭu*) (161; Gen 49:10; Ps 45:7 `שֵׁבֶט מִישֹׁר` for the epilogue's *ḫaṭṭašu išarat*), *agû* = `כתר`; the cognate `חֹטֶר` (Isa 11:1) in the note only.
15. **Cruxes marked `?`:** 218 *nagab uršim* `את מעמקי התושייה?`; 237 *šū igmilu* `הוא אשר חמל?` (Streck reads *ikmilu* "was angry", of Dagan); 267 *mušeppi nābiḫī* `המשתיק את הנובחים?`. SEAL lemma slips recorded in **סטלה**: 1 AN as *šamû*; 164 *erištu* "demand" (= *eršu* "wise"); 262 *wašāru* (= *ešēru* Š); 297 *âru* (= *wâru* D); 18/69/289 *kibru* "bank".
16. *napištu* = `חיים` in *nādin napištim*, *iqīšu napšatam* (202, 210), by the corpus rule (sense).

### Open decisions for the user
1. **`רשע וָזֵד`** for *raggam u ṣēnam* (35; the epilogue repeats it): `זד` is the natural Hebrew for the malicious wicked and makes Mal 3:19–20 visible; the alternative `רשע ורע` would merge *ṣēnu* with *lemnu*.
2. **`קצות הארץ`** for *kibrātum* (vs. `ארבע הפינות` or Isaiah's `ארבע כנפות הארץ`).
3. **`חברו`** for *rū'u* (135), forced by the reserved `רעהו` although *rū'u* is the cognate of `רֵעַ`: keep, or allow `רֵעוֹ` (unreserved form) for this one real cognate.
4. **`קורת הארץ`** (114) for *ṣulūl mātim*, following the shared *ṣulūlu* = `קורה`; it reads stiffly. Alternative `צל הארץ` / `מחסה הארץ` (but *ṣillu* "shade" = `צל` in the epilogue).
5. **`חמורבי`** (renderings §3, still open) – nothing in the prologue argues either way.

- 2026-10-17: read the brief, guidelines, corpus and project renderings and project.md; no earlier LH `>` lines existed (T1 is the first translator). Drafted 1–303 unvocalized, plene, with apparatus, section by section; rank-3 (CDLI, Streck) checked after each line's own reading.
- Review (a) fidelity: every word accounted for; additions in `( )`: 136 `(לו)`, 157 `(תבואה)`, 235 `(ב)`; grammatical `את`/`של`/`על`/`ב` noted where they replace Akkadian case (12, 120, 151, 205, 295); no `[ ]` in the composite's prologue; `⟨ ⟩` at 142, 214.
- Review (b) consistency: identical normalizations identical in Hebrew (5 = 23 `והארץ,`; 142 = 214; 58 ~ 141); each recurring lemma one Hebrew word (list in Proposals §B); names per the tables; vocalized forms strip to plene strings.
- Review (c) Hebrew: read aloud; changed `נתן לו להשיג` → `נתן (לו) להשיג` (136); avoided `משאלה` (root of *šâlu*), `נגיד` (2 Sam 5:2), `נצר` (= *naṣāru*), `מצוק`, `יבול` (Deuteronomic), `עולם`; grep of the `>` lines for `עולם`, `נגח`, `משפט`, `צדק`, `אלהים`, `שמר`, `תורה`, `ברית`, `דעת`, `ברא`, `שבת`, `עבד`, `חוק`: none.
- `python3 -I verify.py 01`: entries 303, not drafted 0, **problems 0**. `check_bible_quotes.py` on the annotated file and project.md: **0 problems** (three fixed: Ezra 5:11 form; two references written as "X של <book>" that the checker could not resolve).

| prol. 115–116 | *mupaḫḫir nišī sapḫātim* | המקבץ את האנשים הנפוצים | `וַתְּפוּצֶינָה מִבְּלִי רֹעֶה` (יחזקאל לד:ה) | [equivalent] | The shepherd gathers the scattered. Our proposal. |
| prol. 115–116 | idem | idem | `וְשָׁב וְקִבֶּצְךָ מִכָּל הָעַמִּים אֲשֶׁר הֱפִיצְךָ` (דברים ל:ג) | [equivalent] | |
| prol. 115–116 | idem | idem | `פֶּן נָפוּץ עַל פְּנֵי כָל הָאָרֶץ` (בראשית יא:ד) | [divergence] | Babylon scatters in Gen; Babylon's king gathers here. Our proposal. |
| prol. 137 | *nizmassu* | את חפצו | `וְכָל חֶפְצִי יַשְׁלִם` (ישעיהו מד:כח) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 139 | *kutî* | כותה | `וְאַנְשֵׁי כוּת עָשׂוּ אֶת נֵרְגַל` (מלכים ב יז:ל) | [equivalent] | Same city, same god (Erra = Nergal, 135). |
| prol. 143 | *rīmum* | הראם | `וְקַרְנֵי רְאֵם קַרְנָיו בָּהֶם עַמִּים יְנַגַּח` (דברים לג:יז) | [cognate] | *rīmu* = `רְאֵם`; goring peoples ↔ 145. |
| prol. 145 | *munakkip zā'irī* | הנוגף את השונאים | `וְכִי יִגֹּף שׁוֹר אִישׁ אֶת שׁוֹר רֵעֵהוּ` (שמות כא:לה) | [cognate] | *nakāpu* = `נגף`; the verb of the goring-ox laws, here a royal epithet. Our proposal. |
| prol. 152 | *ilū šarrī* | אל המלכים | `כִּסְאֲךָ אֱלֹהִים עוֹלָם וָעֶד` (תהלים מה:ז) | [divergence] | The king called "god". |
| prol. 152 | idem | idem | `אֱלָהּ אֱלָהִין וּמָרֵא מַלְכִין` (דניאל ב:מז) | [equivalent] | Said of Daniel's god. |
| prol. 161 | *ḫaṭṭim* | לשרביט | `לֹא יָסוּר שֵׁבֶט מִיהוּדָה` (בראשית מט:י) | [equivalent] | |
| prol. 161 | idem | idem | `שֵׁבֶט מִישֹׁר שֵׁבֶט מַלְכוּתֶךָ` (תהלים מה:ז) | [equivalent] | For the epilogue's *ḫaṭṭašu išarat*. |
| prol. 161 | idem | idem | `וְיָצָא חֹטֶר מִגֵּזַע יִשָׁי` (ישעיהו יא:א) | [cognate] | *ḫaṭṭu* ~ `חֹטֶר` (from memory); meaning differs. |
| prol. 167 | *uṣurātim* | את הצורות | `צוּרַת הַבַּיִת וּתְכוּנָתוֹ` (יחזקאל מג:יא) | [equivalent] | Temple plans. Our proposal. |
| prol. 175 | *mirītim* | מרעה | `בְּמִרְעֶה טּוֹב אֶרְעֶה אֹתָם` (יחזקאל לד:יד) | [cognate] | |
| prol. 175–176 | *mirītim u mašqītim* | מרעה ומשקה | `בִּנְאוֹת דֶּשֶׁא יַרְבִּיצֵנִי עַל-מֵי מְנֻחוֹת יְנַהֲלֵנִי` (תהלים כג:ב) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 176 | *mašqītim* | משקה | `כִּי כֻלָּהּ מַשְׁקֶה` (בראשית יג:י) | [cognate] | |

| prol. 192 | *nīš qātišu* | נשיאת ידו | `מַשְׂאַת כַּפַּי מִנְחַת-עָרֶב` (תהלים קמא:ב) | [cognate] | Prayer as "lifting of the hand". |
| prol. 208 | *lā maḫārim* | שאין לעמוד מולו | `לֹא יִתְיַצֵּב אִישׁ בְּפָנֶיךָ` (דברים ז:כד) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 219–221 | *mušpazzir … ina karašîm* | המסתיר … בכיליון | `יַסְתִּרֵנִי בְּסֵתֶר אָהֳלוֹ` (תהלים כז:ה) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 232 | *mukanniš* | המכריע | `תַּכְרִיעַ קָמַי תַּחְתָּי` (תהלים יח:מ) | [equivalent] | |
| prol. 234 | *purattim* | פרת | `וְהַנָּהָר הָרְבִיעִי הוּא פְרָת` (בראשית ב:יד) | [cognate] | Name. |
| prol. 236 | *bānîšu* | בונהו | `הֲלוֹא הוּא אָבִיךָ קָּנֶךָ הוּא עָשְׂךָ וַיְכֹנְנֶךָ` (דברים לב:ו) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 242–243 | *munawwer pānī Tišpak* | המאיר את פני תישפך | `יָאֵר יְהוָה פָּנָיו אֵלֶיךָ וִיחֻנֶּךָּ` (במדבר ו:כה) | [divergence] | Inverted: the king brightens the god's face. Our proposal. |
| prol. 246–247 | *šāṭip … in pušqim* | המחלץ … בצרה | `עִמּוֹ-אָנֹכִי בְצָרָה אֲחַלְּצֵהוּ` (תהלים צא:טו) | [equivalent] | `מָצוֹק` kept out. Our proposal. |
| prol. 255 | *eli Ištar ṭābā* | על אשתר טובים | `אִם עַל הַמֶּלֶךְ טוֹב` (אסתר ג:ט) | [cognate] | Same idiom "good upon". |
| prol. 260 | *akkade rebītim* | אכד הרחבה | `וְאֶת רְחֹבֹת עִיר` (בראשית י:יא) | [cognate?] | *rebītu* ~ `רְחוֹב`; Rehoboth-Ir explained as *rebīt Ninua*. From memory; uncertain. |
| prol. 261 | *kīnātim* | נכונות | `כִּי לֹא דִבַּרְתֶּם אֵלַי נְכוֹנָה` (איוב מב:ז) | [cognate] | *kīnu* < *kânu*. Our proposal. |
| prol. 262 | *mušūšer ammi* | המיישר את העם | `וְהוּא יְיַשֵּׁר אֹרְחֹתֶיךָ` (משלי ג:ו) | [cognate] | *ešēru* = ישר; *ammu* = `עַם`. |
| prol. 267 | *nābiḫī* | הנובחים? | `כְּלָבִים אִלְּמִים לֹא יוּכְלוּ לִנְבֹּחַ` (ישעיהו נו:י) | [cognate] | Depends on the reading (crux). |
| prol. 268 | *ninua* | נינוה | `וַיִּבֶן אֶת נִינְוֵה` (בראשית י:יא) | [equivalent] | Name. |
| prol. 275 | *liplippim* | צאצא | `וְנֵצֶר מִשָּׁרָשָׁיו יִפְרֶה` (ישעיהו יא:א) | [equivalent] | The just king as dynastic offspring. |
| prol. 279 | *zērum dārium* | זרע נצח | `עַד-עוֹלָם אָכִין זַרְעֶךָ` (תהלים פט:ה) | [equivalent] | |
| prol. 279 | idem | idem | `וַהֲקִימֹתִי אֶת זַרְעֲךָ אַחֲרֶיךָ` (שמואל ב ז:יב) | [equivalent] | |
| prol. 282 | *šamšu bābilim* | שמש בבל | `כִּי שֶׁמֶשׁ וּמָגֵן יְהוָה אֱלֹהִים` (תהלים פד:יב) | [cognate] | Of God in Ps; of the king here. |
| prol. 282 | idem | idem | `לִפְנֵי-שֶׁמֶשׁ יִנֹּין (יִנּוֹן) שְׁמוֹ` (תהלים עב:יז) | [cognate] | The royal psalm. |
| prol. 288 | *muštešmi* | המביא למשמעת | `וּבְנֵי עַמּוֹן מִשְׁמַעְתָּם` (ישעיהו יא:יד) | [cognate] | *šemû* = שמע. Our proposal. |
| prol. 289–290 | *kibrāt arba'im* | קצות הארץ הארבע | `וּנְפֻצוֹת יְהוּדָה יְקַבֵּץ מֵאַרְבַּע כַּנְפוֹת הָאָרֶץ` (ישעיהו יא:יב) | [equivalent] | Also 69–70. |
| prol. 294 | *ana šutēšur nišī* | ליישר את האנשים | `אֹרַח לַצַּדִּיק מֵישָׁרִים יָשָׁר מַעְגַּל צַדִּיק תְּפַלֵּס` (ישעיהו כו:ז) | [cognate] | `ישר`, `מישרים`, `אורח` together, as in 294–299. Our proposal. |
| prol. 295–296 | *mātim ūsim šūḫuzim* | את הארץ באורח להאחיז | `וּנְחֵנִי בְּאֹרַח מִישׁוֹר` (תהלים כז:יא) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 295–296 | idem | idem | `וְהוֹדַעְתָּ לָהֶם אֶת הַדֶּרֶךְ יֵלְכוּ בָהּ` (שמות יח:כ) | [equivalent] | Jethro: teaching the people the way. Our proposal. |
| prol. 297 | *uwa''eranni* | ציווני | `וְכָתַב לוֹ אֶת מִשְׁנֵה הַתּוֹרָה הַזֹּאת עַל סֵפֶר` (דברים יז:יח) | [divergence] | In Deut the king receives the law; here he gives it at the god's command. |
| prol. 298–301 | *kittam u mīšaram ina pī mātim aškun* | אמת ומישרים בפי הארץ שׂמתי | `וְלַמְּדָהּ אֶת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל שִׂימָהּ בְּפִיהֶם` (דברים לא:יט) | [equivalent] | *šakānu* = `שׂם` + "in the mouth". Our proposal. |
| prol. 298–301 | idem | idem | `וּדְבָרַי אֲשֶׁר שַׂמְתִּי בְּפִיךָ` (ישעיהו נט:כא) | [equivalent] | Our proposal. |
| prol. 299 | *mīšaram* | מישרים | `אַתָּה כּוֹנַנְתָּ מֵישָׁרִים` (תהלים צט:ד) | [cognate] | The noun is cognate; the verb differs (*kunnu* there, *šakānu* here). |
| prol. 298–299 | *kittam u mīšaram* | אמת ומישרים | `מֶלֶךְ שׁוֹפֵט בֶּאֱמֶת דַּלִּים כִּסְאוֹ לָעַד יִכּוֹן` (משלי כט:יד) | [equivalent] | Truth, the poor, the throne established forever. Our proposal. |
| prol. 302 | *šīr nišī uṭīb* | את בשר האנשים היטבתי | `לְטוֹב לָנוּ כָּל הַיָּמִים` (דברים ו:כד) | [cognate] | Fulfilment of 47–48. |

### Proposals for the renderings
**A. Names (first occurrence vocalized; plain plene in the `>` lines):** Igigū `אִיגִיגִי`; Anunnakū `אֲנוּנָּכִּי`; Nippur `נִיפּוּר`; Dur-an-ki `דּוּר־אַנְקִי`; Ekur `אֵכּוּר`; Eridu `אֶרִידוּ`; Eabzu `אֵאַבְּזוּ`; Esagil `אֵסַגִּיל`; Ur `אוּר`; Egišnugal `אֵגִּשְׁנוּגַל`; Sippar `סִיפַּר`; Aya `אַיָּה`; *gigunû* `גִּיגוּנוּ` (common noun, transliterated); Ebabbar `אֵבַּבַּר`; Larsa `לַרְסָה`; Uruk `אוּרוּךְ`; Eanna `אֵאַנָּה`; Isin `אִיסִין`; Egalmaḫ `אֵגַלְמַח`; Zababa `זַבָּבָה`; Kiš `כִּישׁ`; Emeteursag `אֵמֵטֵאוּרְסַג`; Ḫursagkalamma `חוּרְסַג־כַּלַמָּה`; Erra `אֵרָּה`; Kutha `כּוּתָה`; Emeslam `אֵמֶסְלַם`; Tutu `טוּטוּ`; Borsippa `בּוֹרְסִפָּה`; Ezida `אֵזִידָה`; Dilbat `דִּלְבַּת`; Uraš `אוּרַשׁ`; Mama `מָמָה`; Keš `כֵּשׁ`; Nintu `נִינְתּוּ`; Lagaš `לַגַשׁ`; Girsu `גִּירְסוּ`; Eninnu `אֵנִנּוּ`; Zabala `זַבָּלָה`; Adad `אֲדַד`; Karkara `כַּרְכַּרָה`; Eudgalgal `אֵאוּדְגַלְגַל`; Adab `אֲדַבּ`; Emaḫ `אֵמַח`; Maškan-šāpir `מַשְׁכַּן־שָׁפִּיר`; Malgium `מַלְגִּיּוּם`; Damkina `דַּמְכִּינָה`; Euphrates `פְּרָת`; Dagan `דָּגַן`; Mari `מָרִי`; Tuttul `תּוּתּוּל` (plene; renderings §3 has `תֻּתּוּל`, which strips to a defective form); Tišpak `תִּישְׁפַּךְ`; Ninazu `נִינַאזוּ`; Eulmaš `אֵאוּלְמַשׁ`; Akkad `אַכַּד`; *lamassu* `לַמַסּוּ`; Assur (city) `אַשּׁוּר`; Nineveh `נִינְוֵה`; Emesmes `אֵמֶסְמֶס`; Sumu-la-el `סוּמוּ־לָה־אֵל`; Sîn-muballiṭ `סִין־מוּבַּלִּיט`; Sumer `שׁוּמֶר`. Same plene rule proposed for `חֻרְסַג` → `חוּרְסַג`, `אֶשְׁנֻנָּה` → `אֶשְׁנוּנָּה`.

**B. Epithets and terms (the epilogue repeats many; plain strings as in the `>` lines):**
- *rē'ûm* `הרועה`; *rē'î nišī* `רועה האנשים`; *nibīt X* `קרוא X`; *šumī ibbû* `בשמי קראו`; *migir X* `רצוי X`; *narām X* `אהוב X`; *talīm X* `האח האהוב של X`.
- *zāninum* `המכלכל`; *na'du* `החסיד`; *pāliḫ ilī* `ירא האלים`; *wašru* `העניו`; *muštēmiqu* `המתחנן`; *muštālu* `המתון`; *gitmālu* `התמים`; *emqu* `החכם`; *eršu* (fem. *erištu*) `הנבון` / `הנבונה`; *lē'û* `היכול` (*lē'ûtu* `יכולת`); *etel šarrī* `אדיר המלכים`; *ašared šarrī* `ראש המלכים`; *ilū šarrī* `אל המלכים`; *ušumgal šarrī* `דרקון־הענק של המלכים`; *šar tašīmtim* `מלך ההבחנה`; *šarrum dannum* `המלך החזק`; *rubûm* `הנסיך`; *qarrādu* / *qurādu* `גיבור`; *qabal lā maḫārim* `קרב / שאין לעמוד מולו`; *ṣulūl mātim* `קורת הארץ` (open); *šamšu bābilim* `שמש בבל`; *zēr šarrūtim* `זרע המלכות`; *zērum dārium* `זרע נצח`.
- Participles: *mukīn* `המכונן`; *mušaklil* `המשכלל`; *mukammer* `הגודש`; *mutēr* `המשיב`; *mubbib* `המזכך`; *mušarbi* `המגדיל`; *muṭīb* (*libbi*) `המטיב (את לב)`; *muḫaddi* `המשמח`; *murīš* `המעליז`; *munēḫ* (*libbi*) `המניח (את לב)`; *muballiṭ* `המחיה`; *mušēṣi nūrim* `המוציא אור`; *munawwer* `המאיר`; *mušēpī* `המופיע`; *mušūšer* `המיישר`; *mupaḫḫir* `המקבץ`; *mušpazzir* `המסתיר`; *šāṭip* `המחלץ`; *mukanniš* `המכריע`; *muštešmi* `המביא למשמעת`; *munakkip* `הנוגף`; *šākin* `השׂם`; *šā'im* `הגוזר`; *nādin* `הנותן`; *bābil* `המוביל`; *āšer* `המשגיח`; *pāqid* `הפוקד`; *mukīl* `המחזיק`; *mušaršid* `המשתית`; *muddiš* `המחדש`; *mušalbiš* `המלביש`; *mušešqi* `המשקה`; *munaḫḫiš* `המשגשג`; *muṭaḫḫid* `המדשן`; *mudeššī* `המדשיא`; *mugarrin* `המערם`; *mušāter* `המרומם`; *muṣīr* `המנשא`; *mullî* `המרים`; *murappiš* `המרחיב`; *mušaddil* `המשגיא`; *muštasḫir* `המקיף`; *mušteṣbî* `המסדיר`; *muštakkin* `השׂם`; *mutammeḫ* `הלוכד`; *mudē* `היודע`; *gāmil* `החומל`; *šēmû* `השומע ל`.
- Nouns: *nuḫšu* `עתרת`; *ḫegallu* `שפע`; *ṭuḫdu* `דשן`; *ḫiṣbu* `תנובה`; *melammu* `זוהר`; *parṣu* `פולחן` (plene; earlier `>` lines have `לפלחן`); *mû* II (cultic orders) `סדרים`; *têrtu* `צו`; *simtu* `ראוי`; *uṣurtu* `צורה`; *nindabû* `מנחה`; *zību* (offering) `זבח` (≠ *zību* "vulture" `עיט`; the flood corpus has *niqû* = `זבח`: if both meet, keep `זבח` for the cognate *zību*); *mākālu* `מאכל`; *ellu* `טהור`; *šuluḫḫu* `טהרה`; *kibrātu* `קצות הארץ`; *dadmū* `יישובים`; *šubtu* `מושב`; *merītu* `מרעה`; *mašqītu* `משקה`; *nizmatu* `חפץ`; *pušqu* `צרה`; *karāšu* `כיליון`; *igigallu* `תבונה`; *ḫaṭṭu* `שבט`; *agû* `כתר`; *ūsu* `אורח`; *kīnātu* `נכונות`; *ammu* `עם` (reserved for it; *nišū* `האנשים`); *rebītu* `רחבה`; *lamassu* `לַמַסּוּ` (transliterated, kept apart from *šēdu* `רוח־מגן`); *ayyābu* and *nakru* both `אויב`, *zā'iru* `שונא`; *rū'u* `חבר` (while `רעהו` is reserved).
- Formulas: *raggam u ṣēnam* `רשע וזד` (open); *dannum enšam ana lā ḫabālim* `שהחזק / בחלש / לא יחבול`; *šīr nišī ṭubbu* `להיטיב את בשר האנשים` / *uṭīb* `היטבתי`; *kittam u mīšaram ina pī mātim aškun* `אמת / ומישרים / בפי הארץ / שׂמתי`; *mīšaram ina mātim šūpû* `מישרים / בארץ / להופיע`; *inūmīšu* `בימים ההם`; *mimma šumšu* `כל אשר לו שם`; *ana ilī rabûtim* `לאלים הגדולים`; *ṣalmāt qaqqadim* `שחורי הראש`; *ešēru* Š/Št `יישר`; *šemû* Št `הביא למשמעת`; *wâru* D `ציווה`; *aḫāzu* Š `האחיז`.

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A reserved word can collide with a real cognate in an unexpected place**: *rū'u* "friend" (prol. 135) is the cognate of `רֵעַ`, but `רעהו` is reserved for the law lines. Reserve by Akkadian lemma when possible ("`רעהו` never for *awīlum*"), not by Hebrew string alone; otherwise record the forced choice.
- **The bible-quote checker resolves a reference only when it follows the quotation in parentheses**: "`X` של <book> c:v" is read as a Genesis reference or as UNRESOLVED. Always write `` `X` (<book> c:v) ``.
- **CDLI's normalization can drop a word that the transliteration has** (prol. 244 *mākalī*) or normalize a Sumerian logogram into an Akkadian phrase (59 DUR.AN.KI → *markas šamê u erṣetim*): translate the `A:` line, note the `N:`.
- **SEAL's lemmas slip most on derived stems** (prol. 262 *mušūšer* as *wašāru*, 297 *uwa''eranni* as *âru*, 164 *erištum* as "demand"): parse the stem before trusting the lemma (guidelines §5).
- **Epithet chains need a verb table before drafting**: the prologue has five verbs of exaltation, four of abundance and three of heaping; fixing one Hebrew verb per Akkadian verb first (Decision 12–13) kept them apart and will let the epilogue reuse them.

## Part T2: the epilogue, part 1 (3144'–3343')

### Scope
- `annotated-11--epilogue-1.rtl.md`: composite lines **3144'–3343'**, all drafted (200 entries, one `>` line each; stele rev. 24:1–24:94, 25:1–25:105, 26:1; SEAL xlvii 1 – xlix 1), in six sections: the title of the laws (3144'–3151'); the shepherd's deeds (3152'–3201'); the stele in Esagil and its purpose (3202'–3239'); the wronged man before the statue (3240'–3295'); the future king guards the words (3296'–3311'); "if that man has discernment" and the closing self-praise (3312'–3343').
- The block ends at a sentence end (3343' *ana emqim ana tanādātim šūṣâ*); 3344' (*šumma awīlum šū ana awâtiya …*, the blessing) begins file 12.
- Base: CDLI P464358 `A:`/`N:`; stele P249253 (slight damage at 3149', 3161', 3163', 3209', 3235', 3256'; 3268' `ik-szu-u4`); SEAL 7520 transliteration and lemmas; CDLI English and Streck read after drafting. No `[ ]` in the composite's lines here.

### Decisions (translator)
1. **3144' *dīnāt*, not SEAL's *kīnāt*.** The stele and CDLI read `di-na-a-at`; SEAL reads `ki-na-a-at` (lemma *kittu*) but Streck translates "Laws". DI and KI are similar signs; *dīnāt* fits *dīn mātim* (3213', 3305', 3318'). Translated `דיני / מישרים` (F38), **no added `(אלה)`**, so as not to manufacture Exod 21:1 `וְאֵלֶּה הַמִּשְׁפָּטִים`; the comparison is in the apparatus and a footnote.
2. **Verbs of giving kept apart:** *nadānu* `נתן`; *šarāku* `מסר` (3155', 3335'); *šutlumu* `הנחיל` (3168'); `העניק` stays T1's *qâšu*, `הקנה` is avoided (*šâmu* = `קנה`). `הנחיל` not used at 3155', so as not to pre-empt Deut 32:8.
3. ***kunnu* = `כונן`** both for the laws (3148' `כונן`) and for setting up the stele (3221' `כוננתי`), as the Akkadian has one verb; *kânu* G stative = `נכון` (3212' `יסודותיו נכונים`).
4. ***šasû* "read (aloud)" = `קרא`** (3248' `יקרא`), although `קרא` is reserved for *nabû* "name" (3184' `קראוני`): Hebrew has no other verb for reading, and *šasû* "call out → read" has exactly the semantic range of `קרא`; this makes Exod 24:7, Deut 17:19, 31:11 visible as [equivalent]. Open decision 1.
5. **The wronged man:** `איש חבול / אשר דבר / יהיה לו,` (F39; *rašû* = `היה ל`, also 3235' `מסלק אל יהיה להן`); Exod 18:16 `כִּי יִהְיֶה לָהֶם דָּבָר` is the same idiom. *kullumu* `הראה` (the stele shows), *amāru* `ראה` (the man sees), *šemû* `שמע`, *napāšu* D `ינפש` (vocalized `יְנַפֵּשׁ`; cognate of Exod 23:12 `וְיִנָּפֵשׁ`).
6. **Pastoral images rendered by meaning, cognates kept:** *aburrī ušarbiṣ* `בנווה / הרבצתי` (Ps 23:2, Ezek 34:14–15); *mugallitu* `מחריד` (Lev 26:6 `וְאֵין מַחֲרִיד`, [equivalent]); *ṣillī ṭābum … tariṣ* `צלי הטוב … פרוש`; *ina utliya* `בחוצני` (`חיק` is reserved for *sūnu*); *rē'ûm mušallimum* `הרועה המשלים` (hif'il, so as not to read "the payer"; F8's root, Isa 44:28 `רֹעִי … יַשְׁלִם`).
7. ***aḫī ul addi*** literally `את זרועי לא השלכתי` (*aḫa nadû* "be negligent"; *nadû* = `השליך`, F14); `הרפה` avoided (*ramû* D). *egû* `התעצל` (`מתרשל` is T1's *naparkû*).
8. ***nakāru* D = `הסיר`** (3309' `אל יסיר`), following corpus §2, though the sense is "alter"; *nasāku* Š = `סילק` (3235' `מסלק`, 3311' `אל יסלק`). Open decision 2.
9. ***damiqtu* = `נאה`** (3237' `שמי בנאה`, as EST `לא נאה`; `טובה` is barred by the *ṭābu* rule); *damāqu* D = `שיפר` (3295' `ישפרו`), since Hebrew has no causative of `נאה` and `היטיב` is *ṭiābu* D. *egerrû* `ניבים` (3289' `את הניבים (עלי)`, the suffix supplied in parentheses).
10. **Other new words:** *kakku* `נשק`; *qablu* `קרב`, *belû* D `כיבה`; *pušqu* `צרה`, *petû* D `פתח`; *qibītu* `מאמר` (*qabû* = `אמר`); *ernittu* `תשועה` (`נצחון` avoided: `נצח` is *dāru*); *nasqu* `נבחר`; *šāninu* `מתחרה`; *bēltu* `גבירה` (EST); *libittu* `לבנה` [cognate, Gen 11:3]; *erēbu* `בא` (`האלים באי אסגיל`); *kibsu* `דרך`, *riddu* `נוהג`; *ḫassu* `משכיל` (*lā ḫassu* `שאינו משכיל`); *rēqu* `ריק`; *tanattu* `תהילה`; *wālidu* `יולד`; *gamru* `שלם` (`בלבו השלם`, like corpus *gummurtu* = `שלמות`, not Deut 6:5's `כל`); *karābu* `ברך` (`יברך אותי`); *warkiāt ūmī* `אחרית הימים` [equivalent]; *ana matīma* `בכל עת`; *bašû* N `יהיה` (not `יקום`, Exod 1:8); *uštaktit* `נכנע?` (meaning uncertain).
11. **SEAL lemma slips** recorded in the apparatus: 3159' *aḫī* as *aḫû* "outsider" (= *aḫu* "arm"); 3325' *lidīn* as *nadānu* (= *diānu*); 3340' *ḫassim* split and lemmatised as *simmu* "wound"; 3296' *warkiāt* split and lemmatised as *arkatu*; 3314' *šutēšuram* as an adjective. **CDLI's normalization** at 3268' adds *uštēšer* (from 3275'): translated from the transliteration.
12. **`verify.py` fixed (one regex):** F1 (`šumma awīlum` → `אם איש`) fired on *šumma awīlum šū*, whose fixed Hebrew is F1 + F2 `אם האיש ההוא`; the pattern now excludes a following *šū* (comment in the script). The curse file needs it too (3344' and every later *šumma awīlum šū*).

### Open decisions for the user
1. **`קרא` for *šasû* "read"** (3248') beside `קרא` for *nabû* "name" (3184'): keep (meaning first; the reading-aloud parallels are visible) or find another verb (none natural in Hebrew).
2. **`הסיר` for *nakāru* D "alter"** (3309', and the curses' *uṣurātiya unakkir*): corpus §2 fixes `הסיר`, but the context is "alter"; `שינה` is *šunnû*, `המיר` is *enû*.
3. **`נכנע?`** for *uštaktit* (3264'), a word of uncertain meaning (CDLI and Streck "submitted himself").
4. **`שמי בנאה`** (3237') for *šumī ina damiqtim*: faithful to the corpus (*damiqtu* = `נאה`) but stiff; the natural `לטובה` is barred by the *ṭābu* rule.

Drafted 3144'–3343' unvocalized, plene, in four chunks, each line read before CDLI/Streck.
- Review (a) fidelity: every word accounted for; additions in `( )`: 3289' `(עלי)`; added `ו` for asyndetic pairs (3204', 3280', 3285', 3293', 3317') per T1's convention; `?` at 3264'; no `[ ]` in the composite; SEAL's and the stele's differences in **סטלה**.
- Review (b) consistency: T1 strings reused letter for letter (`המלך היכול,`, `שהחזק בחלש` / `לא יחבול,`, `לנצח גזר,`, `יום יום`, `האלים הגדולים`, `את לב מרדוך`, `רשע` / `וזד`, `בשר האנשים` idiom, `נכונות`, `הבחנה`, `יכולת`, `תבונה`, `צורה`, `שבט`, `למסו`, `השיג`, `הובלתי` (*wabālu*), `הסתרתי` (*pazāru*)); repeated lines identical (3224' = 3336'; 3307'–3308' = 3319'–3320'; 3233' = 3279' = 3292'; 3280'–3281' = 3293'–3294'; 3220' = 3244' = 3333'; 3260' = 3272'; 3210' = 3236'); the one "identical normalization, different Hebrew" (*ina maḫar* 3278' `ולפני` / 3291' `לפני`) is the *-ma* of 3277', said in the apparatus. Names vocalized at first occurrence; vocalized forms strip to plene (`בְּחוֹצְנִי`, `בְּנָווֶה` fixed at this pass).
- Review (c) Hebrew: read aloud; changed 3289' `ניבי` → `הניבים (עלי)` (no suffix in the Akkadian), 3230' `מישריי` → `המישרים שלי` (keeps F38's string); grepped the `>` lines for reserved words and strings (`שמר`, `משפט`, `צדק`, `תורה`, `ברית`, `עולם`, `נקי`, `אלהים`, `חוק`): none.
- `python3 -I verify.py 11`: entries 200, not drafted 0, **problems 0** (after the F1 regex fix; `verify.py 01` still 0). `check_bible_quotes.py` on the annotated file and project.md: **0 problems** (eight quotations fixed: Zeph 3:13, Isa 1:17, Exod 20:3 and 20:20 (this Bible's numbering), Deut 24:15, Isa 57:19 ketiv/qere, Ps 16:6 and 78:4 forms).

### Proposals for the renderings
**A. New words and formulas (plain strings as in the `>` lines):** *šarāku* `מסר`; *šutlumu* `הנחיל`; *egû* `התעצל`; *aḫa nadû* `את זרועי לא השלכתי` (literal); *pušqī wašṭūtim* `צרות קשות`; *petû* D `פתח`; *kakku* `נשק`; *qablu* `קרב`, *belû* D `כיבה`; *eliš u šapliš* `למעלה ולמטה`; *aburrī* `בנווה` (vocalized `בְּנָווֶה`); *rabāṣu* Š `הרביץ`; *mugallitu* `מחריד`; *rē'ûtu* `רועות` (vocalized `רוֹעוּת`); *rē'ûm mušallimum* `הרועה המשלים`; *ḫaṭṭašu išarat* `שרביטו ישר` (was `שבטו ישר`; 2026-10-23 harmonization); *ṣillu* `צל`; *tarāṣu* `פרוש` (vocalized); *utlu* `חוצן` (vocalized `חוֹצֶן`); *naḫāšu* `שגשג`; *nēmequ* `חכמה`; *ekūtu* `יתומה`; *almattu* `אלמנה`; *purussû* `פסק` (`פסקי הארץ`, `פסקם`); *dīn mātim* `דין הארץ`; *šar mīšarim* `מלך המישרים`; *nasqu* `נבחר`; *šāninu* `מתחרה`; *ul išû* `אין לו/לה/להם`; *qibītu* `מאמר`; *dayyānum rabûm ša šamê u erṣetim* `הדיין הגדול של השמים והארץ`; *nasāku* Š `סילק` (*mušassiku* `מסלק`); *rašû* `היה ל`; *damiqtu* `נאה`; *damāqu* D `שיפר`; *egerrû* `ניב`; *šasû* "read" `קרא` (open); *napāšu* D `ינפש`; *abu wālidu* `אב יולד`; *ernittu* `תשועה`; *bēltu* `גבירה`; *gamru* `שלם`; *karābu* `ברך`; *šēdum lamassum* `רוח־המגן והלמסו`; *ēribūt* `באי`; *libittu* `לבנה` (`לבנת אסגיל`, vocalized `לִבְנַת`); *warkiāt ūmī* `אחרית הימים`; *ana matīma* `בכל עת`; *tašīmtu* `הבחנה`; *qâlu* `הקשיב`; *kibsu* `דרך`, *riddu* `נוהג`; *ḫassu* `משכיל`, *lā ḫassu* `שאינו משכיל`; *rēqu* `ריק`; *tanattu* `תהילה`; *waṣû* Š stative `מוצאים`.

**B. Strings the curse translator (T3, 3344'–3641') must repeat identically** (the epilogue's second half resumes them; grep `annotated-11`):
- *šumma awīlum šū* = `אם האיש ההוא` (F1 + F2; `verify.py` now accepts it); the blessing's *ana awâtiya ša ina narîya ašṭuru iqūlma* = `לדברי אשר במצבתי כתבתי הקשיב,` (cf. 3315'–3316' `לדברים / אשר במצבתי כתבתי יקשיב,`; *qâlu* = `הקשיב`, *lā iqūl* = `לא הקשיב`).
- *narû* `מצבה`; *narî* `מצבתי`; *narî šaṭram* `מצבתי הכתובה`; *šaṭāru* `כתב`; *šumī šaṭram* = `את שמי הכתוב`; *šumšu ištaṭṭar* = `את שמו כתב`; *šumu* `שם`; *zēru* `זרע`.
- *dīnī* = `את דיני`; *dīn mātim ša adīnu* = `את דין הארץ אשר דנתי`; *purussē mātim ša aprusu* = `את פסקי הארץ אשר פסקתי`; *dīnam diānu* `דן דין` (*lidīn* `ידון`), *purussâm parāsu* `פסק פסק`.
- *uṣurātiya* = `את צורותי`; *nakāru* D = `הסיר` (*ay unakkir* `אל יסיר`; *uṣurātiya unakkir* `את צורותי הסיר`; open decision 2); *nasāku* Š = `סילק` (*dīnī ušassik* `את דיני סילק`). For *awâtiya uštepīl* (*šupēlu* "change, distort") T3 needs a third verb: `שינה` (*šunnû*), `המיר` (*enû*), `החליף` (*riābu*) are taken; we suggest `הפך` (`וַיַּהֲפֹךְ … אֶת הַקְּלָלָה לִבְרָכָה`, דברים כג:ו), T3 decides.
- *awâtiya šūqurātim* `דברי היקרים`; *awâtūa* `דברי`; *awât mīšarim* `דברי המישרים`.
- *šar mīšarim* `מלך המישרים`; *ṣalmāt qaqqadim* `שחורי הראש`; *nišī* `האנשים`; *nišīšu* `אנשיו`; *rē'û* / *re'û* `רעה` (*nišīšu ina mīšarim lirʾi* → `את אנשיו במישרים ירעה`); *ḫaṭṭu* `שבט` (now `שרביט`, 2026-10-23 harmonization); *arāku* Š "lengthen" = `האריך` [cognate] ↔ `לְמַעַן יַאֲרִיךְ יָמִים עַל מַמְלַכְתּוֹ` (דברים יז:כ) and `תַּאֲרִיכוּ יָמִים` (דברים לב:מז, after `דָבָר רֵק`, 3341').
- *Šamaš dayyānum rabûm ša šamê u erṣetim* = `שמש, הדיין הגדול של השמים והארץ` (3227'–3229'); *Marduk bēlī* `מרדוך אדוני`; *Zarpanītum bēltī* `צרפניתו גבירתי`; *ilū rabûtum* `האלים הגדולים`; *eliš u šapliš* `למעלה ולמטה`; *nasāḫu* `נסח`; *raggu u ṣēnu* `רשע וזד`.
- *egerrû* `ניבים` + *damāqu* D `שיפר`: Ninlil *mudammiqat igirrêya* → `המשפרת את הניבים (עלי)` (or `את ניבי`, if T3's text has the suffix).
- *šēdum lamassum* `רוח־המגן והלמסו`; *libitti Esagila* `לבנת אסגיל` → *libitti Ebabbar* `לבנת אבבר`.
- *ṭābu* `טוב` only; *damqu*/*damiqtu* `נאה`; *lemnu* `רע`; *dāru*/*dārû* `נצח`.

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **Two fixed formulas can collide in the verification script**: F1 (`šumma awīlum` → `אם איש`) and F2 (`awīlum šū` → `האיש ההוא`) cannot both match *šumma awīlum šū*. When a formula is fixed, list the longer formulas that contain it and test the script on them before drafting.
- **A reserved Hebrew verb can be the only Hebrew for a second Akkadian verb**: `קרא` is reserved for *nabû* "name", but *šasû* "read aloud" has no other Hebrew rendering (and Hebrew `קרא` has exactly *šasû*'s range "call out → read"). Reserve by Akkadian lemma *and sense*, and record such meetings as open decisions instead of forcing a gloss.
- **The epilogue's pastoral and judicial idioms survive in biblical Hebrew word for word** (`הרביץ בנווה`, `אין מחריד`, `יהיה לו דבר`, `דן דין`, `דבר רק`, `ינפש`): render the Akkadian literally first and the biblical phrase appears by itself; the standard English translations ("made lie down in safe pastures", "intimidation", "has a lawsuit", "meaningless") hide every one of them.
- **SEAL splits words across stele lines and lemmatises the halves** (3296' *warkiāt*, 3340' *ḫassim* → "wound"): when a SEAL line ends in a hyphen, join it with the next before reading the lemma.

## Part T3: the epilogue, part 2 (3344'–3641')

### Scope
- `annotated-12--epilogue-2.rtl.md`: composite lines **3344'–3641'**, all drafted (298 entries, one `>` line each; stele rev. 26:2 – 28:91; SEAL xlix 2 – li 91, SEAL lacking l 34–37 = 3479'–3482'), in 16 sections: the blessing (3344'–3359'); the curse protasis (3360'–3386'); Anum (3387'–3394'); Enlil (3395'–3422'); **Ninlil** (3423'–3439'; the stele writes {d}nin-lil₂, not Mullissu); Ea (3440'–3458'); Šamaš (3459'–3485'); Sîn (3486'–3508'); Adad (3509'–3525'); Zababa (3526'–3536'); Ištar (3537'–3573'); Nergal (3574'–3589'); Nintu (3590'–3599'); Ninkarrak (3600'–3619'); the great gods (3620'–3633'); Enlil's closing curse (3634'–3641').
- Base: CDLI P464358 `A:`/`N:`; stele P249253 (differences at 3361' `<a-na>`, 3378' `er-re-szum!`, 3387' AN, 3405' two emended signs, 3480', 3488' **šēressu for têressu**, 3540', 3554', 3567' `-ma?`, 3586' TI, 3601', 3609' `a-zu!`, 3619' `da!`, 3635' `da!`), SEAL 7520 transliteration and lemmas, CDLI English and Streck read after drafting. No `[ ]` in the composite's lines here.

### Decisions (translator)
1. **EST reused letter for letter where the Akkadian is the same in form** (Decision 9): *arratam maruštam … līrurū* `מארה` / `נמרצת יָאוֹרוּ` (3632'–3633' = EST 475); *līrur* `יָאוֹר` / `תָּאוֹר` (3394', 3548', 3639'); *mušīm šīmātim* `גוזר הגורלות` (3396' = EST 414); *ana šīmtim lišīm* `לגורל` / `יגזור לו` (3413'–3414', 3507'–3508' = EST 434); *eklet lā nawārim* `חשכת` / `ללא הארה` (3410'–3411' = EST 485–486); *eliš ina balṭūtim lissuḫšu šapliš ina erṣetim eṭemmašu mê lišaṣmi* `למעלה, / מן החיים / יִסַּח אותו; / למטה, / בארץ, / את אִטּוֹ / למים יַצְמִיא.` (3479'–3485' = EST 476–477, singular; `בארץ` for *erṣetu* where EST has `בקרקע` for *kaqqiru*); *ḫušaḫḫu* `כפן`, *bubūtu* `רעב` (EST 480); *tānēḫu* `אנחה`, *murṣu* `מחלה` (EST 461); *simmu* `פצע`, *zumru* `גוף` (EST 462); *kīma mê* `כמים`; *šebēru* `שבר` (EST 453, 573); *maqātu* Š `הפיל`; *rēmu* `רחמים`; *ṣēru* `ערבה` (EST 426); *qamû* `ילהט` (EST 525, after EST's harmonization away from `קלה`); *ṭīdu* `טיט` (EST 609); *aplu* `יורש` (EST 433); *gugal šamê u erṣetim* `מפקח התעלות של השמים` / `והארץ,` (EST 440); *nagbu* `מעמקים` (EST 521); *Ištar bēlet tāḫazim u qablim* `אשתר, גבירת` / `המלחמה והקרב,` (EST 453 has the reverse order `הקרב והמלחמה`, as its Akkadian); *ezzu* `עז`, *aggu* `זועם` (EST *aggiš* `בזעם`); *pašāṭu* `מחה` (EST 661); *ḫulluqu* `יְאַבֵּד` (EST 661); *eṭemmu* `אִט`. Each reuse is noted "= אמנת אסרחדון N" in the apparatus.
2. **T2's strings repeated** (Proposals §B): `אם האיש ההוא`; `לדברי` / `אשר במצבתי כתבתי` / `הקשיב,` (and `לא הקשיב,`); `את דיני לא סילק,`; `את צורותי` / `הסיר` (*nakāru* D, T2 open decision 2, extended to Dt *lā uttakkaru* = `לא יוּסַר`, 3398', 3638'); `את שמי הכתוב` / `מחה,` / `ואת שמו כתב,`; `מלך המישרים,`; `את אנשיו` / `במישרים ירעה.`; `שבט`; `האלים הגדולים`; `רוח־המגן`; `לבנת אבבר`; `המשפרת` / `את ניבי` (the suffix is in the text); `השיג`; `תשועה`; `הלמסו שלי`; `נשק`; `קרב`; `מאמר`; `שמש, הדיין הגדול` / `של השמים` / `והארץ,`.
3. ***šupêlu* = `הפך`** (T2's suggestion): `לא הפך` (3350'), `הפך` (3372'), `את מלכותו להפוך` (3417', "change his dynasty"; Hag 2:22 `וְהָפַכְתִּי כִּסֵּא מַמְלָכוֹת`). Hence *sakāpu* "overthrow" = `הדף` (3465').
4. ***eṭēru* "deprive" = `שלל (מן)`** throughout (`ישלול ממנו` ×4, `תשלול ממנו`): `לקח` (*leqû*), `הסיר` (*nakāru*), `גרע` (Deut 4:2; 13:1) are taken or reserved.
5. ***šipṭu* = `המשפט`** (3429' `במקום המשפט`), by corpus renderings §5 ("reserve `מִשְׁפָּט` for *šipṭu*"). The project's reserved list (§4) says "there is no *šipṭu* in LH" – there is, here; the string `המשפט` is not in `verify.py`'s list, so the check passes. Eccl 3:16 `מְקוֹם הַמִּשְׁפָּט` is the same phrase [cognate]. See open decision 1.
6. **One Akkadian root, one Hebrew family where possible:** *ešû* "confuse" family – *tēšû* (3401'), *mīšītu* (3450'; CDA "confusion" < *ešû*, not *mišītu* "oblivion"), *ešītu* (3555') all `מבוכה`; the verb *ešû* (3468') `שיבש`. `מהומה` (Deut 28:20) kept out: *tēšû* is its [equivalent], not its cognate. *damāmu* `נהה` / *dimmatu* `נהי`; *šanānu* Gt `מתחרים` (T2 *šāninu* `מתחרה`); *banû* `בנה` (`האל בּוֹנִי`, `האם בּוֹנָתִי`, `אל תבנה` – T1 `בּוֹנֵהוּ`; ↔ Deut 25:9, Gen 16:2); *marṣu* `נמרץ` (EST 475; `הדבר הנמרץ`, `פצע נמרץ`); *kabtu* `כבד` (`בפיו הכבד`, `עון כבד`, `מחלה כבדה`, `נכבד`).
7. **Army words kept apart:** *ummānu* `חיל` (creation corpus: not `צבא`, Gen 2:1), pl. `חילותיו`; *ṣābu* (ERIN₂, 3629') ~~`גדודיו`~~ `צבאו` (coordinator, harmonized with T8's 731); *qarrādu* `גיבור`; *nišū* `אנשים`. Battle words: *qablu* `קרב`, *tāḫāzu* `מלחמה` (EST), *tamḫāru* `מערכה` (new).
8. **Idioms rendered by the Hebrew idiom where the Akkadian literal would mislead:** *ana qāt nakrīšu limallīšu* (lit. "fill him into the hand") = `ביד אויביו` / `תסגיר` (Ps 31:9; Lam 2:7) – `מילא יד` is priestly consecration (Exod 28:41), `נתן ביד` would collide with *nadānu* = `נתן`, `מסר` is *šarāku*; *emēdu* (impose a penalty) = `ישית עליו` (Exod 21:22, 30); *ina pī Enlil šarrim lišaškin* = `בפי אנליל / המלך / תָּשִׂים` (*šakānu* = `שׂם`; Num 23:5 `וַיָּשֶׂם יְהוָה דָּבָר בְּפִי בִלְעָם`); *awassu lilemmin* = `את דברו תרשיע` (Deut 25:1; the root of *lemnu* `רע` gives no natural verb); *tabāk napištišu kīma mê* = `הגרת נפשו` / `כמים` (2 Sam 14:14; `שפך` is reserved for *šapāku*).
9. **Literal where the literal is the finding:** *šumam ay ušaršīšu* `ושם אל תמציא לו` (the "name" is the son, Deut 25:6; CDLI and Streck "offspring" hide it); *zēr awīlūtim … ay ibni* `זרע אדם` / `אל תבנה` (Deut 25:9; CDLI "allow", Streck "engender"); *lā šubšâm*, *ay ušabši* `לבלי הביא לכלל הוויה`, `אל יביא לכלל הוויה` (creation-corpus *šubšû*; CDLI/Streck "blot out" is a paraphrase, not *pašāṭu*); *mūt niṭil īnim* `מות מראה העין` (sudden death or blindness: both in the note); *šaknat napištim* `את אשר נפש שׂוּמה בו` (Gen 1:30 `אֲשֶׁר בּוֹ נֶפֶשׁ חַיָּה`); *šīram lemnam* `בשר רע` (the ominous flesh of extispicy); *asûm qerebšu lā ilammadu* `רופא את קרבו` / `לא ילמד` (*lamādu* = `למד`).
10. **Other new words:** *mêšu* `בזה` (Num 15:31); *adāru* `פחד` (`ירא` is *palāḫu*); *pasāsu* `ביטל` (`הפר`, Lev 26:15, in the apparatus only); *iššiakku* `מושל`; *awīlūtu* `אדם`; *palû* `שלטון`; *gabaraḫḫu* `חרדה?`; *napāḫu* Š `הפיח?` (3405', reading uncertain); *šubbû* `השקיט`; *zikru* `זכר`; *lapātu* Š `הרס`; *šīmtu* pl. `גורלות`; *ina maḫra alāku* `בראש הלך`; *apkallu* `חכם` (shared corpus rendering; collides with T1/T2 *emqu* `החכם` – noted); *uznu* `בינה` (creation corpus); *warû* `נהג` (`ינהג אותו`, `כפות תנהג אותו`; Deut 28:37 `יְנַהֶגְךָ`, 4:27); *nagbu* `מעמקים`; *sekēru* `סכר` (Gen 8:2); *ašnan* `דָּגָן` (creation corpus, always vocalized against the god `דָּגַן`); *tukultu* `מבטח`; *urḫu* `נתיב` (`אורח` is T2's *ūsu*; the cognate in the note); *neḫelṣû* Š `המעיד`; *bīru* `בדיקה` (`קסם` kept for the apparatus, Deut 18:10); *šūpû* stative `מופיע`; *têrtu* `צו`; *šērtu* `עונש`; *arnu* `עון` (F37); *warḫu* `ירח` [cognate]; *kammālu* `יריב`; *naṭālu* Š `יַרְאֶה` (open decision 3); *zunnu* pl. `גשמים`; *mīlu* `גאות` (flood corpus); *šasû* "roar" `צעק` (guidelines §5; T2's *šasû* "read" = `קרא` stays for reading); *tīl abūbim* `תל מבול` (*tīlu* = `תל` [cognate], Deut 13:17); *izuzzu* Š `העמיד`; *imnu* `ימין`; *uzzatu* `עברה`; *saḫmaštu* `מרידה` (`מרד` is corpus *sīḫu*); *qurunnu* `ערמה`; *šalamtu* `גוויה` (`פגר` = *pagru*); *nukurtu* `איבה`; *kamîš* `כפות`; *kašūšu* `מַפָּץ` (Enūma eliš; the flood corpus has `השמדה`); *apu* `אגם` (reed-marsh, Jer 51:32; EST *appāru* `אגמון`); *naṭû* Š `הכה` (`הכה` is barred only for *maḫāṣu*); *binītu* `איברים`; *ḫabāšu* `ריסק`; *dumqu* `נאה` (`נאה עלי`, as EST 279); *asakku* `אָסַכּוּ` (flood corpus); *pašāḫu* `נרגע` (creation corpus); *ṣimdu* `חבישה`; *nâḫu* D `הניח`; *nišku* `נשיכה` [cognate]; *belû* G `כבה`; *eṭlūtu* `בחרות`; *napḫaru* `כלל`.
11. **Grammar:** goddesses take feminine verbs (Ninlil, Ištar, Nintu, Ninkarrak); *-ma* is `ו` on the next line where it links clauses (3367', 3377', 3450', 3524', 3535', 3567', 3572', 3587', 3595', 3616', 3640'), a comma elsewhere; *ina nagbim* is `במעמקים` (3453', where the rivers are dammed) and `מן המעמקים` (3515', whence the high water comes): `verify.py` lists the pair for review – the difference is the two senses of *ina*.
12. **Readings recorded:** 3488' composite *têressu* translated (`צוויו`), the stele's *šēressu* "his penalty" (SEAL, Streck) in **סטלה**; 3405' *lišappiḫ* read as *napāḫu* Š (SEAL lemma *šapāḫu* "sprinkle" unlikely); 3459' CDLI `N:` *šama* is a typo for *šamaš*; 3567' CDLI `N:` drops the transliteration's *-ma*; SEAL lemma slips: 3484' *utukku* for GIDIM₄ (= *eṭemmu*), 3624' *aḫu* for ŠU.NIGIN (= *napḫaru*).

### Open decisions for the user
1. **`המשפט` for *šipṭu*** (3429', *ašar šipṭim* `במקום המשפט`): corpus §5 reserves `משפט` for exactly this word, but the project's reserved list (§4) bars `משפט`/`משפטים` in the `>` lines on the assumption that LH has no *šipṭu*. Keep `המשפט` (the cognate; Eccl 3:16), or use `שפיטה` (same root, not the Torah's word). If kept, `verify.py`'s list should state the exception (now it passes only because `המשפט` with the article is not listed).
2. **`מבוכה` for *tēšû* / *mīšītu* / *ešītu*** (3401', 3450', 3555'): `מהומה` is the more natural Hebrew for *tēšû* "chaos, political disorder" and is the word of Deut 28:20 beside `הַמְּאֵרָה` – i.e. LH has the same pair (*erretu*, *tēšû*). Kept out by echo control; the user may prefer `מהומה` with the tag.
3. **`יַרְאֶה` for *naṭālu* Š** (3503' `יריב למלכות / יַרְאֶה לו`): `הראה` is reserved for *kullumu* (corpus §2), but Hebrew has no other causative of seeing.
4. **`חרדת?` for *gabaraḫḫu*** (3402'; CDA "panic?", CDLI and Streck "rebellion") and **`יָפִיחַ?`** for *lišappiḫaššum* (3405'): both uncertain; alternatives in the apparatus.
5. **`חכם` for *apkallu*** (3443', shared corpus rendering) beside T1/T2's *emqu* `החכם` (prol., 3342'): two Akkadian words in one Hebrew word; the coordinator may move *emqu* to `משכיל`? – but T2 has *ḫassu* `משכיל`.
6. **`ביטל` for *pasāsu*** (3370'): post-biblical Hebrew; `הפר` (Lev 26:15; Num 15:31) is the exact biblical verb, kept out so as not to pre-empt Lev 26:15.

### Proposals for the renderings
**A. Curse formulas (plain strings as in the `>` lines; most = EST):** *erretu* `מארה` (`את מארותי`, `וממארות האלים`, `בגלל המארות ההן`, `במארות` / `האלה`); *erretam maruštam līrurū* `מארה` / `נמרצת יאורו`; *līrur* `יאור` (fem. `תאור`), *līruršu* `יאור אותו`; *mušīm šīmātim* `גוזר הגורלות`; *ana šīmtim lišīmšum* `לגורל` / `יגזור לו`; *līṭeršu* `ישלול ממנו`; *lišbir* `ישבור` / `תשבור`; *kakkašu lišbir* `את נשקו ישבור`; *ḫaṭṭašu lišbir* `את שרביטו ישבור` (was `את שבטו ישבור`; 2026-10-23 harmonization); *eliš … ina balṭūtim lissuḫšu, šapliš ina erṣetim eṭemmašu mê lišaṣmi* `למעלה, מן החיים יסח אותו; למטה, בארץ, את אטו למים יצמיא`; *eklet lā nawārim* `חשכת ללא הארה`; *ina ḫušaḫḫim u bubūtim* `בכפן וברעב`; *ḫalāq ālišu / nišīšu / mātišu* `אבדן עירו / אנשיו / ארצו`; *šumšu u zikiršu* `את שמו ואת זכרו`; *lā šubšâm*, *ay ušabši* `לבלי הביא לכלל הוויה`, `אל יביא לכלל הוויה`; *ina pīšu kabtim* `בפיו הכבד`; *ša lā uttakkaru* `אשר לא יוסר`; *arḫiš likšudāšu* `מהרה ישיגוהו`; *awatum maruštum* `הדבר הנמרץ`; *ana qāt nakrīšu limallīšu* `ביד אויביו תסגיר`; *ana māt nukurtišu kamîš līrūšu* `לארץ איבתו כפות תנהג אותו`; *rēmam ay ušarši* `רחמים אל תמציא`; *aplam līṭeršu* `יורש ישלול ממנו`; *šumam ay ušaršīšu* `שם אל תמציא לו`; *zēr awīlūtim ay ibni* `זרע אדם אל תבנה`; *murṣam kabtam* `מחלה כבדה`; *simmam marṣam* `פצע נמרץ`; *asûm qerebšu lā ilammadu* `רופא את קרבו לא ילמד`; *ūmī warḫī šanāt* `ימים, ירחים, שנות`; *ina tānēḫim u dimmatim* `באנחה ובנהי`; *māssu ana til abūbim litēr* `את ארצו לתל מבול ישיב`; *ūmam ana mūšim litēr* `את היום ללילה ישיב`.
**B. Names and epithets:** Anum *rabûm abu ilī* `אנו הגדול, אבי האלים`; Enlil *bēlum mušīm šīmātim* `אנליל, האדון, גוזר הגורלות`; Ninlil `נִינְלִיל` *ummum rabītum* `האם הגדולה` (not Mullissu: the stele has the Sumerian name); Ea *rubûm rabium … apkal ilī mudē mimma šumšu* `אאה, הנסיך הגדול … חכם האלים, היודע כל אשר לו שם`; Šamaš *dayyānum rabium ša šamê u erṣetim … bēlum tukultī* `שמש, הדיין הגדול של השמים והארץ … האדון, מבטחי`; Sîn *bēl šamê ilum bānî* `סין, אדון השמים, האל בּוֹנִי`; Adad *bēl ḫegallim gugal šamê u erṣetim rēṣūa* `אדד, אדון השפע, מפקח התעלות של השמים והארץ, עוזרי`; Zababa *qarrādum rabium mārum rēštûm ša ekur āliku imniya* `זבבה, הגיבור הגדול, הבן הראשון של אכור, ההולך לימיני`; Ištar *bēlet tāḫazim u qablim* `אשתר, גבירת המלחמה והקרב`; Nergal *dannum ina ilī qabal lā maḫār* `נרגל, החזק באלים, קרב שאין לעמוד מולו`; Nintu *bēltum ṣīrtum ša mātātim ummum bānītī* `נינתו, הגבירה הנעלה של הארצות, האם בּוֹנָתִי`; Ninkarrak *mārat anim* `נינכרך, בת אנו`; *Anunnakū ina napḫarišunu* `האנונכי בכללם`; *šēd bītim* `רוח־המגן של הבית`; *Ašnan* `דָּגָן` (always vocalized).
**C. New single words:** see Decision 10 (*šipṭu* `המשפט` subject to open decision 1).
**D. For `verify.py` (coordinator):** add an exception for *šipṭ* → `המשפט` (or decide open decision 1), and consider listing `מהומה`, `מטר`, `שחין`, `דבר` (plague), `קללה` as reserved in the epilogue files (they were kept out by hand here).

### Lessons for the guidelines
- **A reserved list built on "word X does not occur in this text" must be checked against the whole text**: the project list bars `משפט` because "there is no *šipṭu* in LH", but *šipṭu* occurs once, in the curses (3429'), and the corpus rule reserves `משפט` exactly for it. Before reserving a Hebrew word by absence, grep the normalization of every file for the cognate lemma.
- **The rank-3 translations paraphrase exactly the verbs that carry the parallels**: "offspring" for *šumu* (the name = the son, Deut 25:6), "engender / allow" for *banû* (Deut 25:9 `יִבְנֶה`), "blot out" for *lā šubšû*, "deliver" for *ana qāt mullû*, "slip" / "undermine" for *neḫelṣû*. Translate the verb first, and only then read the English.
- **Curse lists repeat across a millennium word for word** (*arratam maruštam … līrurū*, *eliš … šapliš … eṭemmu … mê*, *eklet lā namāri*, LH c. 1750 → EST 672 BCE): a curse translator should grep the earlier corpus's `N:` lines (not the Hebrew) for every curse phrase before drafting – the script `pairs.py`-style grep (normalization → `>` line) found 25 reusable strings in minutes.
- **Keep one Hebrew family for one Akkadian root even when the Akkadian uses three nouns** (*tēšû*, *mīšītu*, *ešītu* < *ešû* → `מבוכה`): the reader then sees that the curses of Enlil, Ea and Ištar repeat one idea.
- **The bible-quote checker needs the reference immediately after the backticked quotation**: "`X` של דברים כח:טו" resolves to Genesis; and this Bible numbers Deut 5:15 (= 5:16) and Jer 31:26 (= 31:27) differently.

## Part T4: §1–5, §14–25, §117–126

### Scope
- `annotated-02--laws-1-25.rtl.md` (new): **§1–§5** (304–376, 73 lines) and **§14–§25** (506–618, 113 lines) = **186 entries**, one `>` line each, one `## §N (first–last)` section per law; the placeholder `<!-- טרם תורגם: §6–§13 -->` between §5 and §14 (129 lines for T7).
- `annotated-05--laws-101-126.rtl.md` (new): **§117–§126** (1324'–1450', **127 entries**); the placeholder `<!-- טרם תורגם: §101–§116 -->` at the start of the sections (194 lines for T8).
- Base: CDLI P464358 `A:`/`N:`; stele P249253 (differences at 324, 376 `usz-ta-ab`, 536, 537, 601, 615 `il-te-di`; 1325'/1345' `e-hi-il-tum`, 1350'–1351', 1396' `a-sar`, 1419' (CDLI's stele numbering skips 4:72), 1431'–1432', 1434', 1436'). No `[ ]` in the composite here. CDLI English read after drafting; Harper 1904 grepped for §1–5, 14–25, 117–126.

### Decisions (translator)
1. **The formulas exactly as renderings §1** (F1–F43), split across lines where the Akkadian splits them: F12 `לפני` / `האל` (587–588); F13 `את חיי האל` / `יזכיר` (564–565); F15 `לעדות` / `שקרים` (337–338); F18 `לדין ההוא` / `תביעה` / `אין.` (1398'–1400', in the Akkadian order); F20 `ואת הדבר אשר אמר` / `לא כונן` (340–341), *uktīnšu* `ולא כונן עליו` (308, 314), *ukannūšu* `יכוננו עליו` (363, 1410'); F23 `בידו` / `נתפש` (554–555); F33 `אל הנהר יצלול`, `הנהר השיגו`, `הנהר זיככו`, `ויצא שלם`; F35 `דין דן`, `פסק` / `פסק`, `תעודה חתומה`, `מכסא` / `דיינותו`, `ובאספה`; F36 `דין נפשות`; F37 `את עון` / `הדין ההוא` / `ישא`.
2. ***nērtu* = `רצח`** (306): *dâku* is `הרג` in this corpus (F3), so the noun of the other root (*nêru*) gets `רצח`; corpus §2 says "there is no *nêru*" – there is, in the noun. Tagged [equivalent] ↔ Exod 20:12 (this Bible's numbering), Num 35:30.
3. ***ubburu* "accuse" = `האשים`**, *mubbiru* = `מאשים` (305, 309, 322, 333; §126 1440' *babtašu ūtebbir* `את רובעו האשים`). `העליל` (Deut 22:14) kept for the apparatus (it prejudges the falsity).
4. ***nadû* "cast (a charge)" = `הטיל`** (307, 313, 329), stative/N `הוטלו` (316); *nadû* N "be thrown" `יושלך` (618).
5. ***tabālu* = `נטל`** (323, 334 `יטול`): `לקח` is *leqû*, `נשא` *našû*, `הוביל` *wabālu*.
6. **Id (the divine River)** = `הנהר` (F33), with a note on the DINGIR; `אל הנהר יצלול` also where the Akkadian has no *ana* (319).
7. ***enû* = `המיר`** (corpus §2; 359, 362 `בהמירו`); *ezēbu* Š (issue a document) `השאיר` (357); *tebû* Š "remove (from office)" `יקימוהו` (372); *bašû* N `היה` (366, 1357').
8. ***lū … lū* = `בין … בין`** (corpus §3), ***u lū*** (single) = `או` (522, 1329', 1338', 1358', 1361'), ***u lū … u lū*** = `בין … ובין` (1418'–1419').
9. **Slaves:** *ḫalqu* of persons `נמלט` (520, 532; corpus *ḫalāqu* = `נמלט` / `אבד`); *raqû* `החביא` (`הסתיר` is *pazzuru*); *šisīt nāgirim* `לקריאת הכרוז`; *redû* `הוליך`; *kalû* `כלא`; *warkatam parāsu* `עניינו ייפסק` (*warkatu* = `עניין`, *parāsu* = `פסק` as F35); *ṣābitānu* `תופש`; ***wuššuru* Dt = `שוחרר`** (566 `וישוחרר`), so that ***paṭāru* = `פטר`** (1351' `יפטור`, the cognate; EST also `פטר`).
10. **§21 crux *iḫallalūšu*** = `ויתלוהו?` (CDLI/Roth "hang"; eBL/CDA *alālu* II), the alternative "wall him in / confine" (*ḫalālu* I; Harper) in the apparatus and a footnote; *palāšu* `פלש` (vocalized), *pilšu* `מפלש` (F/renderings §2).
11. ***mimmû* (noun) = `רכוש`** (585, 596, 1415', 1420'–1421', 1430', 1435', 1437', 1441'): **deviation from F22** (*mimmûm ḫalqum* `דבר אבוד`), because `דבר` is fixed for *awatum* (corpus §0; 340) – `את רכושו האבוד`. *mimma* (pronoun) = `כל` (`כל אשר`, `וכל אשר לו שם` = T1's *mimma šumšu*). ***makkūru* = `נכסים`** (1427' `ולבעל הנכסים`; proposal for §6 *makkūr ilim u ekallim* `נכסי האל וההיכל`).
12. **City and quarter:** *ālum u rabiānum* `העיר וראש העיר`; *erṣetu* `ארץ` (592 `אשר בארצם`), *paṭṭu* `גבול` (593); *ḫabātu* / *ḫubtu* / *ḫabbātu* / *ḫabtu* = `גזל` / `גזלה` / `גזלן` / `גזול`; *bābtu* `רובע` (vocalized `רוֹבְעוֹ`).
13. **Fire (§25):** `אש נופחה` (vocalized `נוּפְּחָה`); *bullû* `לכבות` (T2 *belû* D `כיבה`); *numātu* `כלי` (vocalized `כְּלֵי`); *īnam našû* `את עינו נשא` (literal, = Gen 39:7).
14. **Debt slavery (§117–119):** *e'iltu* `חובה` (vocalized), *ṣabātu* `תפשתו`; ***kiššātu* = `שעבוד`, *kāšišu* = `משעבד`** (one Hebrew root for one Akkadian root, *kašāšu*); *šāyyāmānu* `קונה` (`קוֹנָם` vocalized against `קונם` "vow"); *epēšu* `יעשו (מלאכה)` – not `יעבדו` (Exod 21:2's verb); *ina rebūtim šattim* `ברביעית` / `שנה` (the ordinal precedes in the Akkadian and the words are on two lines); *etēqu* Š `יעביר` (keeps both readings, "let the term pass" / "transfer"); ***baqāru* N = `לא יעורער`** (EST *paqāru* `לערער`); *walādu* `ילד`.
15. **Storage and deposit (§120–126):** *našpakūtu* `אחסנה`, *našpaku* `אסם`, *qarītu* `ממגורה`, *šapāku* `שפך`, *ibbû* `חסרון`, *gamru* `לגמרי`; ***nakāru* G "deny" = `התנכר (ל־)`** – the cognate (Gen 42:7), not `כחש` (Lev 5:21–22, kept for the apparatus) and not `כפר` (reserved); ***šanû* Št = `הכפיל`** (`שינה` is *šunnû*); *bâru* D `ברר`; *idū* `דמי` (`שכר` would read as *šikaru* `שכר`); *kurru* `כור`, *qû* `קב` (`קבים`); *ašar* `במקום אשר`; *nabalkattu* `טיפוס`; *egû* `התעצל` (T2; `התרשל` is T1's *naparkû*); *ḫulluqu* `איבד`; *še'û* Gtn `יחפש`; *šarrāqānu* `גנבו` (vocalized `גַּנָּבוֹ`); *ragāmu* `תבע` (with *rugummû* `תביעה`); *kīma* "that" `כי` (1441', not F7's `כ`).
16. **Translated from the transliteration where CDLI's `N:` differs:** 506 (`N:` *awīlim*, typo for *awīlum*); 522 (`N:` drops *u*); 1361' and 1418' (`A:` *ana*, `N:` *ina*). Recorded in the apparatus.
17. Identical normalizations with different Hebrew (verify.py review list): *ana maṣṣarūtim* `ולנצירה` (1390') and *bēl bītim* `ובעל הבית` (1367') carry the *-ma* of the preceding line; both noted in the apparatus.

### Open decisions for the user
1. **`ויתלוהו?`** (§21, 574) or the alternative `ויסגרוהו?` / "wall him in": the reading decides whether Deut 21:22 (`וְהוּמָת וְתָלִיתָ אֹתוֹ עַל עֵץ`) is a real sequence parallel.
2. **`רצח` for *nērtu*** (306): the natural word and the noun of Exod 20:12 `לֹא תִרְצָח` [equivalent]; the alternative `הריגה` collides with *dâku* = `הרג`.
3. **`התנכר` for *nakāru* "deny (a deposit)"** (1364', 1397', 1408', 1411'): the cognate, but the Hebrew sense ("disown, act as a stranger") is broader; the alternative `כיחש` is the Torah's own verb (Lev 5:21–22) and would pre-empt the comparison.
4. **`רכוש` for *mimmû*** (against F22 `דבר אבוד`): the coordinator should update F22 to `רכוש אבוד` for T7 (§9–§13), or decide otherwise.
5. **`יעשו (מלאכה)`** for *ippešū* (1334'): faithful to *epēšu* but the added word is needed for Hebrew; `יעבדו` would be smoother and is Exod 21:2's verb.
6. **`שעבוד` / `משעבד`** for *kiššātu* / *kāšišu*: Mishnaic, exact in both senses (lien and servitude); the alternative `עבדות חוב` loses the root link.

- `python3 -I verify.py 02 05`: entries 313, not drafted 323 (the placeholders §6–§13, §101–§116), **problems 0**; review list: the two *-ma* cases (Decision 17). `check_bible_quotes.py` on both files and project.md: **0 problems** (fixed: Ps 122:5 `שָׁמָּה`; Exod 21:8 shortened to avoid the ketiv/qere; the Mishnah reference and two short references that the checker read as Genesis; Ezek 45:14 form).

| §3, 345 | *awīlum šū iddâk* | האיש ההוא יהרג | `וְדָרְשׁוּ הַשֹּׁפְטִים הֵיטֵב וְהִנֵּה עֵד שֶׁקֶר הָעֵד שֶׁקֶר עָנָה בְאָחִיו וַעֲשִׂיתֶם לוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר זָמַם לַעֲשׂוֹת לְאָחִיו` (דברים יט:יח–יט) | [equivalent] | The false witness in a capital case dies, as the accused would have; LH has no inquiry clause and no talion list (Deut 19:21). From memory. |
| §4, 349–351 | *aran dīnim šuāti ittanašši* | את עון הדין ההוא ישא | `וְהוּא עֵד אוֹ רָאָה אוֹ יָדָע אִם לוֹא יַגִּיד וְנָשָׂא עֲוֹנוֹ` (ויקרא ה:א) | [equivalent] | F37; *arnu* ~ `עון`, *našû* = `נשא` [cognate]. Both concern a witness. From memory. |
| §4, 351 | *ittanašši* | ישא | `וַעֲשִׂיתֶם לוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר זָמַם לַעֲשׂוֹת לְאָחִיו` (דברים יט:יט) | [equivalent] | The false witness pays what was at stake in the case. |
| §5, 352 | *dayyānum* | דיין | `אֲבִי יְתוֹמִים וְדַיַּן אַלְמָנוֹת` (תהלים סח:ו) | [cognate] | |
| §5, 352 | *dayyānum* | דיין | `שֹׁפְטִים וְשֹׁטְרִים תִּתֶּן לְךָ בְּכָל שְׁעָרֶיךָ` (דברים טז:יח) | [equivalent] | The Torah's judge is `שֹׁפֵט` (cognate of *šapāṭu*). |
| §5, 353 | *dīnam idīn* | דין דן | `דִּין לֹא דָנוּ דִּין יָתוֹם` (ירמיהו ה:כח) | [cognate] | |
| §5, 359 | *dīnšu iteni* | את דינו המיר | `לֹא תַטֶּה מִשְׁפָּט לֹא תַכִּיר פָּנִים וְלֹא תִקַּח שֹׁחַד` (דברים טז:יט) | [divergence] | The Torah forbids perverting judgment (bribery, partiality); LH punishes *altering* a sealed verdict, no motive named. From memory. |
| §5, 359 | idem | idem | `לֹא תַטֶּה מִשְׁפַּט אֶבְיֹנְךָ בְּרִיבוֹ` (שמות כג:ו) | [divergence] | Also Exod 23:7–8 (bribery). |
| §5, 359 | *enû* | המיר | `לֹא יַחֲלִיפֶנּוּ וְלֹא יָמִיר אֹתוֹ` (ויקרא כז:י) | [equivalent] | Corpus *enû* = `המיר`. |
| §5, 367 | *adi šinšerīšu* | עד פי שנים עשר | `שְׁנַיִם יְשַׁלֵּם` (שמות כב:ג) | [equivalent] | The highest multiple in LH. |
| §5, 370 | *ina kussîm dayyānūtišu* | מכסא דיינותו | `כִּי שָׁמָּה יָשְׁבוּ כִסְאוֹת לְמִשְׁפָּט` (תהלים קכב:ה) | [cognate] | *kussû* = `כסא`. |
| §5, 376 | *itti dayyānī ina dīnim ul uššab* | עם הדיינים בדין לא ישב | `לֹא תַכִּירוּ פָנִים בַּמִּשְׁפָּט כַּקָּטֹן כַּגָּדֹל תִּשְׁמָעוּן לֹא תָגוּרוּ מִפְּנֵי אִישׁ כִּי הַמִּשְׁפָּט לֵאלֹהִים הוּא` (דברים א:יז) | [divergence] | The Torah instructs the judge; the removal from office, the 12-fold fine and the assembly are [absent] in the Torah. From memory. |
| §5, 376 | idem | idem | `צֶדֶק צֶדֶק תִּרְדֹּף` (דברים טז:כ) | [divergence] | |
| §5, 376 | idem | idem | `כִּי לֹא לְאָדָם תִּשְׁפְּטוּ כִּי לַיהוָה` (דברי הימים ב יט:ו) | [equivalent] | Royally appointed judges, as in LH. Our proposal. |
| §14, 507 | *mār awīlim* | בן איש | `גַּם בְּנֵי אָדָם גַּם בְּנֵי אִישׁ` (תהלים מט:ג) | [equivalent] | The class term (F16). |
| §14, 508 | *ṣeḫram* | צעיר | `וְגֹנֵב אִישׁ וּמְכָרוֹ וְנִמְצָא בְיָדוֹ מוֹת יוּמָת` (שמות כא:טז) | [divergence] | LH protects only the young child of the free class; Exodus any man. *ṣeḫru* = `צעיר` [cognate]. |
| §14, 509 | *ištariq* | גנב | `וְגֹנֵב אִישׁ וּמְכָרוֹ וְנִמְצָא בְיָדוֹ מוֹת יוּמָת` (שמות כא:טז) | [equivalent] | Kidnapping = "stealing" a person in both; death in both. From memory (Paul 1970). |
| §14, 509 | idem | idem | `כִּי יִמָּצֵא אִישׁ גֹּנֵב נֶפֶשׁ מֵאֶחָיו מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְהִתְעַמֶּר בּוֹ וּמְכָרוֹ וּמֵת הַגַּנָּב הַהוּא` (דברים כד:ז) | [divergence] | Deut adds the sale and limits to "his brothers". |
| §15, 512 | *warad ekallim* | עבד ההיכל | `לֹא תַסְגִּיר עֶבֶד אֶל אֲדֹנָיו אֲשֶׁר יִנָּצֵל אֵלֶיךָ מֵעִם אֲדֹנָיו` (דברים כג:טז) | [divergence] | LH protects the owner (palace, *muškēnum*); the Torah the runaway. |
| §15, 514 | *muškēnim* | מסכן | `טוֹב יֶלֶד מִסְכֵּן וְחָכָם` (קהלת ד:יג) | [cognate loan] | A status ("commoner"), not "poor": note. |
| §15, 514 | idem | idem | `וּמָצָא בָהּ אִישׁ מִסְכֵּן חָכָם` (קהלת ט:טו) | [cognate loan] | |
| §15, 516 | *abullam uštēṣi … iddâk* | דרך השער הוציא, יהרג | `לֹא תַסְגִּיר עֶבֶד אֶל אֲדֹנָיו אֲשֶׁר יִנָּצֵל אֵלֶיךָ מֵעִם אֲדֹנָיו. עִמְּךָ יֵשֵׁב בְּקִרְבְּךָ` (דברים כג:טז–יז) | [divergence] | The direct opposite: death for helping a slave escape (§15–16, 19), a reward for returning him (§17) vs. "do not hand him over". From memory (standard). |
| §16, 520 | *ḫalqam* | נמלט | `וַיִּבְרְחוּ שְׁנֵי עֲבָדִים לְשִׁמְעִי אֶל אָכִישׁ בֶּן מַעֲכָה מֶלֶךְ גַּת` (מלכים א ב:לט) | [equivalent] | And 2:40: Shimei fetches them back – the ANE extradition practice that Deut 23:16 forbids. Our proposal. |
| §16, 526 | *nāgirim* | הכרוז | `וְכָרוֹזָא קָרֵא בְחָיִל` (דניאל ג:ד) | [equivalent] | |
| §16, 528 | *bēl bītim* | בעל הבית | `וְנִקְרַב בַּעַל הַבַּיִת אֶל הָאֱלֹהִים` (שמות כב:ז) | [cognate] | F17. |
| §17, 533 | *ina ṣērim* | בערבה | `כִּי בַשָּׂדֶה מְצָאָהּ` (דברים כב:כז) | [equivalent] | "In the open country", outside the city. |
| §17, 539 | *šinā šiqil kaspam … inaddiššum* | שני שקלי כסף … יתן לו | `לֹא תַסְגִּיר עֶבֶד אֶל אֲדֹנָיו` (דברים כג:טז) | [absent] | A reward for returning a runaway: absent (and forbidden) in the Torah. |
| §18, 546 | *warkassu ipparras* | עניינו ייפסק | `וְדָרְשׁוּ הַשֹּׁפְטִים הֵיטֵב` (דברים יט:יח) | [equivalent] | Investigation of the case. |
| §18, 548 | *ana bēlišu utarrūšu* | ולאדוניו ישיבוהו | `לֹא תַסְגִּיר עֶבֶד אֶל אֲדֹנָיו` (דברים כג:טז) | [divergence] | The palace itself returns the slave. |
| §19, 554–555 | *ina qātišu ittaṣbat* | בידו נתפש | `אִם הִמָּצֵא תִמָּצֵא בְיָדוֹ הַגְּנֵבָה` (שמות כב:ג) | [equivalent] | F23. |
| §19, 554–555 | idem | idem | `וְגֹנֵב אִישׁ וּמְכָרוֹ וְנִמְצָא בְיָדוֹ` (שמות כא:טז) | [equivalent] | |
| §20, 564–565 | *nīš ilim izakkar* | את חיי האל יזכיר | `שְׁבֻעַת יְהוָה תִּהְיֶה בֵּין שְׁנֵיהֶם אִם לֹא שָׁלַח יָדוֹ בִּמְלֶאכֶת רֵעֵהוּ` (שמות כב:י) | [equivalent] | The custodian from whom the slave / the animal escaped swears and is released. F13. |
| §20, 564 | *nīš ilim* | חיי האל | `חֵי פַרְעֹה` (בראשית מב:טו) | [equivalent] | Oath "by the life of". |
| §20, 566 | *utaššar* | וישוחרר | `וְלָקַח בְּעָלָיו וְלֹא יְשַׁלֵּם` (שמות כב:י) | [equivalent] | The oath clears him of liability. |
| §21, 569 | *bītam ipluš* | בית פלש | `אִם בַּמַּחְתֶּרֶת יִמָּצֵא הַגַּנָּב וְהֻכָּה וָמֵת אֵין לוֹ דָּמִים` (שמות כב:א) | [equivalent] | *palāšu* ~ `חתר` (reserved); `פלש` is the Aramaic/Mishnaic cognate. |
| §21, 569 | idem | idem | `חָתַר בַּחֹשֶׁךְ בָּתִּים` (איוב כד:טז) | [equivalent] | The housebreaker digs through a mud-brick wall. |
| §21, 573 | *idukkūšu* | יהרגוהו | `אִם זָרְחָה הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ עָלָיו דָּמִים לוֹ` (שמות כב:ב) | [divergence] | The Torah: no bloodguilt at night, bloodguilt by day; LH: the breaker is sentenced to death, no day/night distinction (Eshnunna §12–13 has it, from memory). |
| §21, 574 | *iḫallalūšu* | ויתלוהו? | `וְכִי יִהְיֶה בְאִישׁ חֵטְא מִשְׁפַּט מָוֶת וְהוּמָת וְתָלִיתָ אֹתוֹ עַל עֵץ` (דברים כא:כב) | [equivalent] | Kill, then hang: the same sequence – **only if** the crux is read "hang" (CDLI, CDA *alālu*); "wall in" (Harper) has no such parallel. Our proposal. |
| §21, 574 | idem | idem | `וַיְמִיתֵם וַיִּתְלֵם עַל חֲמִשָּׁה עֵצִים` (יהושע י:כו) | [equivalent] | Same condition. |
| §21, 574 | idem | idem | `וַיַּהַרְגוּם וַיְקַצְּצוּ אֶת יְדֵיהֶם וְאֶת רַגְלֵיהֶם וַיִּתְלוּ עַל הַבְּרֵכָה בְּחֶבְרוֹן` (שמואל ב ד:יב) | [equivalent] | Same condition; the killed are exposed at a public place. |

| §22, 580 | *iddâk* | יהרג | `וְשִׁלַּם אֹתוֹ בְּרֹאשׁוֹ וַחֲמִשִׁתָיו יֹסֵף עָלָיו` (ויקרא ה:כד) | [divergence] | Restitution + a fifth vs. death. From memory. |
| §23, 582 | *ḫabbātum lā ittaṣbat* | הגזלן לא נתפש | `אִם לֹא יִמָּצֵא הַגַּנָּב וְנִקְרַב בַּעַל הַבַּיִת אֶל הָאֱלֹהִים` (שמות כב:ז) | [equivalent] | The thief not found → before the god. |
| §23, 587–589 | *maḫar ilim ubâr* | לפני האל יברר | `עַד הָאֱלֹהִים יָבֹא דְּבַר שְׁנֵיהֶם` (שמות כב:ח) | [equivalent] | F12; `לפני האל`, never `אלהים`. |
| §23, 586 | *ḫalqam* | האבוד | `עַל כָּל אֲבֵדָה` (שמות כב:ח) | [equivalent] | F22. |

| §23, 592–593 | *ša ina erṣetišunu u paṭṭišunu* | אשר בארצם ובגבולם | `וּמָדְדוּ אֶל הֶעָרִים אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבֹת הֶחָלָל` (דברים כא:ב) | [equivalent] | Responsibility by territory. |
| §23, 598 | *iriabbūšum* | יחליפו לו | `כִּי יִמָּצֵא חָלָל בָּאֲדָמָה … לֹא נוֹדַע מִי הִכָּהוּ` (דברים כא:א) | [divergence] | Unknown offender → the city: LH compensates the victim; Deut 21 expiates by the heifer and the elders' declaration. From memory. |
| §24, 599 | *napištum* | נפש | `וְלֹא תִקְחוּ כֹפֶר לְנֶפֶשׁ רֹצֵחַ` (במדבר לה:לא) | [divergence] | LH: the city pays a mina for the life; the Torah: no money for a life. |
| §24, 603 | *išaqqalū* | ישקלו | `כֶּסֶף יִשְׁקֹל כְּמֹהַר הַבְּתוּלֹת` (שמות כב:טז) | [cognate] | F6. |
| §25, 606 | *išātum innapiḫ* | אש נופחה | `תְּאָכְלֵהוּ אֵשׁ לֹא נֻפָּח` (איוב כ:כו) | [cognate] | *napāḫu* = `נפח`. |
| §25, 606 | idem | idem | `כִּי תֵצֵא אֵשׁ וּמָצְאָה קֹצִים` (שמות כב:ה) | [equivalent] | |
| §25, 612 | *īnšu iššī* | את עינו נשא | `וַתִּשָּׂא אֵשֶׁת אֲדֹנָיו אֶת עֵינֶיהָ אֶל יוֹסֵף` (בראשית לט:ז) | [cognate] | The same idiom of the covetous look. Our proposal. |
| §25, 612 | idem | idem | `לֹא תַחְמֹד בֵּית רֵעֶךָ` (שמות כ:יג) | [equivalent] | This Bible's numbering (= Exod 20:17). |
| §25, 618 | *ana išātim šuāti innaddi* | לאש ההיא יושלך | `כִּי תֵצֵא אֵשׁ וּמָצְאָה קֹצִים וְנֶאֱכַל גָּדִישׁ … שַׁלֵּם יְשַׁלֵּם הַמַּבְעִר אֶת הַבְּעֵרָה` (שמות כב:ה) | [divergence] | Two fire laws, different subjects: the Torah's fire-setter pays; LH's looter is thrown into the same fire. |
| §25, 618 | idem | idem | `הַנִּלְכָּד בַּחֵרֶם יִשָּׂרֵף בָּאֵשׁ אֹתוֹ וְאֶת כָּל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ` (יהושע ז:טו) | [equivalent] | The taker of forbidden goods burnt. Our proposal. |

| §117, 1326' | idem | idem | `וְהִנֵּה אֲנַחְנוּ כֹבְשִׁים אֶת בָּנֵינוּ וְאֶת בְּנֹתֵינוּ לַעֲבָדִים` (נחמיה ה:ה) | [equivalent] | |
| §117, 1327'–1328' | *aššassu mārašu u mārassu ana kaspim iddin* | את אשתו, את בנו ואת בתו נתן בכסף | `וְכִי יִמְכֹּר אִישׁ אֶת בִּתּוֹ לְאָמָה לֹא תֵצֵא כְּצֵאת הָעֲבָדִים` (שמות כא:ז) | [divergence] | In LH wife, son and daughter all go out in the 4th year; in Exodus the daughter-*amah* "does not go out"; sale of a *wife* is [absent] in the Torah. From memory. |
